### [62] [Near-Tight Approximation Algorithms for Bottleneck Multiple Knapsack Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05233)
*Lin Chen,Tingwei Hu,Yuchen Mao,Yong Chen,Lili Mei,An Zhang,Guangting Chen,Guochuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究了瓶颈多背包问题，当knapsack容量相同时给出接近最优的(2/3 - ε)-近似算法；当容量任意时给出(1/2 - ε)-近似算法并证明(1/2 + ε)- hardness，两种情况下的近似比均几乎达到紧界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究瓶颈多背包问题的近似算法设计，目标是在容量约束下最大化每个knapsack获得的最小利润，这是一个具有实际应用价值的组合优化问题。

Method: 采用近似算法设计技术，针对两种knapsack容量场景分别设计：(1) 相同容量场景给出(2/3 - ε)-近似；(2) 任意容量场景给出(1/2 - ε)-近似。

Result: 相同容量knapsack：(2/3 - ε)-近似（几乎匹配(2/3 + ε)不可近似下界）；任意容量knapsack：(1/2 - ε)-近似且证明(1/2 + ε)不可近似。

Conclusion: 本文提出的算法在两种场景下均接近最优近似比：当knapsack容量相同时，几乎达到了不可近似下界(2/3 + ε)；当容量任意时，与下界(1/2 + ε)仅相差一个常数ε。

Abstract: In the bottleneck multiple knapsack problem, we are given a set of items and a set of knapsacks, where each item has a profit and a weight, and each knapsack has a capacity. Our goal is to assign items to knapsacks so as to maximize the minimum profit received by any knapsack subject to the capacity constraint.
  When all knapsacks have identical capacity, we give a $(\frac{2}{3} - \varepsilon)$-approximation algorithm for any constant $\varepsilon > 0$. This result almost matches the $(\frac{2}{3} + \varepsilon)$ inapproximability bound for the bottleneck multiple subset sum problem (Caprara et al., 2000).
  When the knapsacks can have arbitrary capacities, we propose a $(\frac{1}{2} - \varepsilon)$-approximation algorithm for any constant $\varepsilon > 0$. We also prove a hardness bound of $(\frac{1}{2} + \varepsilon)$ for any constant $\varepsilon > 0$.

</details>


### [63] [Algorithmic Phase Transition for Large Independent Sets in Dense Hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05618)
*Abhishek Dhawan,Nhi U. Dinh,Eren C. Kızıldağ,Neeladri Maitra,Bayram A. Şahin*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究密集随机超图中寻找大独立集的算法可行性。在稀疏情形下，局部/低阶多项式(LDP)框架有效；但密集情形需要根本不同的范式。研究两类模型：r-一致Erdős-Rényi超图和γ平衡r-部分超图中的独立集问题。设计了达到r^(1/(r-1))和(max_i γ_i)^(-1/(r-1))逼近比的在线算法，并证明这些逼近比是最优的——任何在线算法都无法突破这些下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The sparse regime uses local/LDP frameworks, but the dense regime requires fundamentally different algorithmic paradigms. Even for classical Erdős-Rényi graphs, LDPs are conjectured to fail in regimes accessible to online algorithms, necessitating new approaches.

Method: Design of online algorithms for both models, combined with hardness reductions proving lower bounds.

Result: Precise threshold values for the size of largest independent sets in both models, with online algorithms achieving multiplicative approximation factors of r^(1/(r-1)) (uniform) and (max_i γ_i)^(-1/(r-1)) (r-partite).

Conclusion: Two online algorithms achieve optimal approximation ratios: r^(1/(r-1)) for uniform hypergraphs and (max_i γ_i)^(-1/(r-1)) for r-partite hypergraphs, with matching lower bounds proving no online algorithm can do better.

Abstract: We study the algorithmic tractability of finding large independent sets in dense random hypergraphs. In the sparse regime, much of the natural algorithms can be formulated within either the local or the low-degree polynomial (LDP) framework, and a rich literature has subsequently identified nearly sharp algorithmic thresholds within these classes by exploiting their stability. In the dense setting, however, the algorithmic paradigms are fundamentally different: they are online and thus need not be stable. Perhaps more crucially, even for the classical Erdős-Rényi random graph $G(n,p)$, LDPs are conjectured to fail in the 'easy' regime accessible to online algorithms, thereby challenging their viability for dense models.
  Our focus is on two models: (i) finding large independent sets in dense $r$-uniform Erdős-Rényi hypergraphs, and (ii) the more challenging problem of finding large $γ$-balanced independent sets in dense $r$-uniform $r$-partite hypergraphs, where the $i$-th coordinate of $γ\in\mathbb{Q}^r$ specifies the proportion of vertices from $V_i$ in the independent set. For both models, we pinpoint the size of the largest independent set and design online algorithms that achieve a multiplicative approximation factor of $r^{1/(r-1)}$ in the uniform and $(\max_i γ_i)^{-1/(r-1)}$ in the $r$-partite model. Furthermore, we establish matching algorithmic lower bounds, showing that these computational gaps are sharp: no online algorithms can breach these gaps.

</details>


### [64] [Label Correcting Algorithms for the Multiobjective Temporal Shortest Path Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05954)
*Edina Marica,Clemens Thielen,Alina Wittmann*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究单源多目标时序最短路问题在目标函数不满足单调性和等渗性条件下的求解方法，通过引入最大路径长度约束K并开发标签校正算法克服零时长循环带来的挑战，证明了在特定条件下算法可计算所有非支配解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法依赖于目标函数的单调性（推广非负性假设）和等渗性（保证路径扩展时目标值顺序不变），但在更一般的多目标优化场景中这些假设难以满足，需要研究无此假设的问题求解方法。

Method: 开发了通用标签校正算法处理带最大路径长度K限制的问题变体，并建立了若干充分条件证明在特定条件下无需K界约束即可获得全部非支配解。

Result: 在无单调性和/或等渗性假设下，通过引入最大路径长度K的约束实现了对非支配解的有效搜索，并证明了在特定条件下无需K界算法即可完整求解。

Conclusion: 提出了一般标签校正算法，并为不需要界K的充分条件提供了理论支撑，证明了算法能够计算所有非支配图像。

Abstract: Given a directed, discrete-time temporal graph $G=(V,R)$, a start node $s\in V$, and $p\geq1$ objectives, the single-source multiobjective temporal shortest path problem asks, for each $v\in V$, for the set of nondominated images of temporal $s$-$v$-paths together with a corresponding efficient path for each image. A recent general label setting algorithm for this problem relies on two properties of the objectives - monotonicity and isotonicity. Monotonicity generalizes the nonnegativity assumption required by label setting methods for the classical additive single-objective shortest path problem on static graphs, while isotonicity ensures that the order of the objective values of two paths is preserved when both are extended by the same arc.
  In this paper, we study the problem without assuming monotonicity and/or isotonicity. A key difficulty in this setting is that zero-duration temporal cycles may need to be traversed an arbitrary finite number of times to generate all nondominated images. This motivates the study of a restricted problem variant in which a maximum admissible path length $K$ is imposed, and only paths containing at most $K$ arcs are considered. We develop general label correcting algorithms for this setting and establish several sufficient conditions under which such a bound is not required, implying that the algorithms compute all nondominated images.

</details>


### [65] [The Pareto Frontier of Randomized Learning-Augmented Online Bidding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06106)
*Mathis Degryse,Imrane Saakour,Christoph Dürr,Spyros Angelopoulos*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究了带oracle预测的随机在线竞价问题，提出bidding function统一框架，给出了一致性-鲁棒性权衡的紧致界（R≥2.885时），并通过增量中位数实验验证了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线竞价是online decision-making领域的经典问题，广泛应用于资源分配、分层聚类和近似算法分析。然而，learning-augmented variant下的随机在线竞价算法的理论极限尚不清晰，尤其是 consistency 与 robustness 之间的权衡关系存在理论gap。

Method: 提出并形式化了 bidding function 概念，作为随机竞价策略设计与分析的统一框架。基于该框架，推导了一致性 C 关于鲁棒性 R 的上界和下界，并通过巧妙的构造证明了这些界的紧致性。

Result: 给出了 C(R) 的解析上界与下界，当 R ≥ 2.885 时上下界匹配，完整刻画了该权衡关系。同时将算法应用于增量中位数问题，实验表明该方法在实用聚类场景中有效。

Conclusion: 通过提出 bidding function 抽象框架，解决了先前工作中存在的不一致性问题。当鲁棒性 R ≥ 2.885 时，上下界匹配，有效关闭了理论gap。此外，通过增量中位数问题的实验验证了该算法在实际聚类场景中的适用性。

Abstract: Online bidding is a classical problem in online decision-making, with applications in resource allocation, hierarchical clustering, and the analysis of approximation algorithms. We study its randomized learning-augmented variant, where an online algorithm generates a sequence of random bids while leveraging predictions from an oracle. We provide analytical upper and lower bounds on the optimal consistency $C$ as a function of the robustness $R$, which match when $R \geq 2.885$, effectively closing the gap left by previous work. The key technical ingredient is the notion of a bidding function, a novel abstraction that provides a unified framework for the design and analysis of randomized bidding strategies. We complement our theoretical results with an experimental application of randomized bidding to the incremental median problem, demonstrating the applicability of our algorithm in practical clustering settings.

</details>


### [66] [On the Parameterized Approximability of (Mergeable) Sum of Radii Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06398)
*Ameet Gadekar*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文研究了k球半径和问题(k-MSR)的参数化复杂性和算法设计。证明k-MSR是W[2]-难的，排除了高效参数化近似方案；同时给出FPT (8/3+ε)-近似算法，改进自(4+ε)，并通过合适赋值子程序达到(2+ε)-近似，可应用于多种公平性和隐私约束聚类问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k-MSR问题尽管已被广泛研究，但在参数化复杂性理论中的精确位置仍不清楚，且是否存在高效参数化近似方案（EPAS）这一基础问题未得到解答。同时，现有的约束聚类变体的参数化算法性能也有提升空间。

Method: 1. 硬度方面：通过从支配集问题的归约证明W[2]-硬度，并基于此排除EPAS和sub-exponential时间算法。2. 算法方面：基于可合并约束框架，利用集合覆盖/覆盖算法的技术，结合问题结构设计迭代式聚类方法，获得常数因子FPT近似。

Result: 1. 硬度结果：k-MSR是W[2]-难的，排除了EPAS（除非W[2]=FPT），且在ETH假设下排除了在f(k,ε)·n^{o(k)}时间内运行的算法。2. 算法结果：获得FPT (8/3+ε)-近似，改进了之前的(4+ε)上界；借助合适的赋值子程序可达到(2+ε)-近似，并推广到(t,k)-fair、(α,β)-fair、ℓ-diversity和私有聚类等问题。

Conclusion: 该研究完成了k-MSR问题的参数化复杂性定位，并提供了FPT近似算法的新记录。在约束聚类问题上达到与无约束问题相同的(2+ε)近似比，是一个重要的理论突破。

Abstract: The sum of radii problem ($k$-MSR) asks, given a metric space on $n$ points, to place $k$ balls covering all points so as to minimize the sum of their radii. Despite extensive study from the perspectives of approximation and parameterized algorithms, the exact parameterized complexity of the problem and the existence of efficient parameterized approximation schemes remained open. We advance this understanding on both the hardness and algorithmic fronts.
  We begin by showing that $k$-MSR is $W[2]$-hard parameterized by $k$, thereby pinpointing its location in the $W$-hierarchy. Moreover, via our reduction, we rule out efficient parameterized approximation schemes (EPAS)--that is, $(1+ε)$-approximations running in time $f(k,ε)\cdot \mathrm{poly}(n)$--unless $W[2] = FPT$. Assuming the Exponential Time Hypothesis, we further rule out such algorithms running in time $f(k,ε)\cdot n^{o(k)}$, strengthening recent lower bounds for the problem.
  On the algorithmic side, we study $k$-MSR under the framework of mergeable constraints, which captures a broad class of clustering constraints, including fairness, diversity, and lower bounds. We obtain an FPT $(\frac{8}{3}+ε)$-approximation, improving upon the previous best guarantee of $(4+ε)$. Moreover, given access to a suitable assignment subroutine, we achieve a $(2+ε)$-approximation, matching the best known bound for the unconstrained problem. This, in turn, yields $(2+ε)$ FPT-approximations for several important settings, including $(t,k)$-fair, $(α,β)$-fair, $\ell$-diversity, and private clustering.

</details>


### [67] [Fast decremental tree sums in forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06555)
*Benjamin Aram Berendsohn,Marek Sokołowski*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究两类增量动态图问题（tree-sum和subtree-sum查询），在边删除场景下通过微-宏树分解等技术将查询复杂度降至O(log* n)，无权重时达O(1)，并证明存在渐进普遍最优算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准动态森林数据结构（如top trees）对删除、更新和查询操作均需O(log n)时间；研究者希望探究删除专用问题是否能够更快解决。

Method: 采用Alstrup等人(1997)的微-宏树分解技术，结合最优小规模实例的预计算数据结构，并深入分析OPT行为；对于子树和查询问题通过建立下界和匹配上界证明紧致性。

Result: tree-sum查询达O(log* n)每操作（无权重时O(1)）；群模型下达到O(OPT(F,m))的渐进普遍最优；子树和查询获得多种变体的紧致复杂度界。

Conclusion: 对于tree-sum查询，提出了基于微-宏树分解的数据结构，将每操作时间从O(log n)降至O(log* n)，在无权重情况下可达O(1)；对于子树和查询，给出了多种变体的紧致界；证明了在群模型下存在渐进普遍最优算法，处理m个操作的时间复杂度为O(OPT(F, m))。

Abstract: We study two fundamental decremental dynamic graph problems. In both problems, we need to maintain a vertex-weighted forest of size $n$ under edge deletions, weight updates, and a certain information-retrieval query. Both problems can be solved in $O(\log n)$ time per update/query using standard dynamic forest data structures like top trees, even if additionally edge insertions are allowed. We investigate whether the deletion-only problem can be solved faster.
  First, we consider $\texttt{tree-sum}$ queries, where we ask for the sum of vertex weights in one of the connected components (i.e., trees) in the forest. We give a data structure with $O(n)$ preprocessing time and $O(\log^* n)$ time per operation, based on a micro-macro tree decomposition (Alstrup et al., 1997). If the forest is unweighted (i.e., all weights are 1 and cannot be changed), then the operation time can be improved to $O(1)$.
  Additionally, we give an asymptotically universally optimal algorithm. More specifically, our algorithm works in the group model, and processes $m$ operations on an initial forest $F$ in running time $O( \mathrm{OPT}(F, m) )$. Here $\mathrm{OPT}(F, m)$ is the number of weight additions and subtractions that a best possible algorithm performs to handle a worst-case instance for a fixed initial forest $F$ and a fixed number $m$ of operations. We achieve this with a combination of the aforementioned decomposition technique, precomputation of optimal data structures for very small instances, and some insights into the behavior of $\mathrm{OPT}$. Note that even the worst-case complexity of this algorithm remains unknown to us.
  Second, we consider $\texttt{subtree-sum}$ queries. Here, the forest is rooted, and a query $\texttt{subtree-sum}(v)$ returns the sum of weights in the subtree rooted at $v$. We show tight bounds for several variants of this problem. [...]

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [68] [LLMorphism: When humans come to see themselves as language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05419)
*Valerio Capraro*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: LLMorphism指将人类认知类比为大型语言模型的偏误信念。随着对话式AI的普及，这种偏误通过类比迁移和隐喻可得性两种机制传播开来。本文将其与相关概念区分，并探讨其对多个社会领域的深远影响，指出公共讨论往往忽视了对人类心智的贬低问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着对话式LLM的兴起，人们可能从"人工系统产生类人语言"这一现象反向推论"人类思维像LLM"，这种推断具有偏误性，因为语言输出的相似性并不必然意味着认知架构的相似性。

Method: 区分LLMorphism与机械论、拟人论、计算主义、去人性化、具身化以及预测加工心智理论等概念；分析其传播的两个机制——类比迁移（将LLM特征投射到人类）和隐喻可得性（LLM词汇成为描述思维的文化显著词汇）。

Result: LLMorphism对工作、教育、责任、医疗保健、沟通、创造力和人类尊严等多个领域产生影响，涉及边界条件和抵抗形式的讨论。

Conclusion: 公共讨论可能遗漏了问题的一半：问题不仅是是否对机器过度归因心智，也包括是否开始对人类过度归因心智缺失。LLMorphism的边界条件和抵抗形式值得进一步探讨。

Abstract: LLMorphism is the biased belief that human cognition works like a large language model. I argue that the rise of conversational LLMs may make this bias increasingly psychologically available. When artificial systems produce human-like language, people may draw a reverse inference: if LLMs can speak like humans, perhaps humans think like LLMs. This inference is biased because similarity at the level of linguistic output does not imply similarity in cognitive architecture. Yet, LLMorphism may spread through two mechanisms: analogical transfer, whereby features of LLMs are projected onto humans, and metaphorical availability, whereby LLM vocabulary becomes a culturally salient vocabulary for describing thought. I distinguish LLMorphism from mechanomorphism, anthropomorphism, computationalism, dehumanization, objectification, and predictive-processing theories of mind. I outline its implications for work, education, responsibility, healthcare, communication, creativity, and human dignity, while also discussing boundary conditions and forms of resistance. I conclude that the public debate may be missing half of the problem: the issue is not only whether we are attributing too much mind to machines, but also whether we are beginning to attribute too little mind to humans.

</details>


### [69] [The Pedagogy of AI Mistakes: Fostering Higher-Order Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05472)
*Hadi Hosseini*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: This paper reframes AI limitations as educational opportunities by integrating AI into a database design syllabus to foster critical thinking and higher-order cognitive skills.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Generative AI's inherent errors and hallucinations, typically viewed as drawbacks, can serve as deliberate triggers for critical thinking and reflection when properly structured in course design.

Method: Mixed-methods approach examining structured AI interaction in a database design course; analysis focuses on metacognitive engagement, disciplinary rigor, and perceived competency outcomes.

Result: Investigation of how AI-generated errors support metacognitive engagement, reinforce disciplinary rigor, and relate to students' AI literacy and subject-matter competency in database design education.

Conclusion: The paper argues that AI limitations can be systematically leveraged as pedagogical tools rather than mere obstacles, contributing to higher-order cognitive development aligned with established learning frameworks.

Abstract: As generative AI becomes increasingly integrated into higher education, its frequent errors and hallucinations, often seen as limitations, offer a unique pedagogical opportunity. By framing AI as a ``learning companion'' whose imperfect outputs prompt analysis, evaluation, and reflection, we argue that instructors can engage students in the fundamental processes of higher-order thinking. This paper presents a design-oriented study in which an AI-integrated syllabus in a \textit{database design} course deliberately leverages AI's limitations to foster critical thinking and higher-order cognitive skills aligned with Bloom's taxonomy of learning. Using a mixed-methods approach, we examine how structured interaction with AI-generated errors supports metacognitive engagement, reinforces disciplinary rigor, and relates to students' perceived AI literacy and subject-matter competency.

</details>


### [70] [Rigorous Interpretation Is a Form of Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05508)
*Isabelle Lee,Emmy Liu,Cathy Jiao,Brihi Joshi,Dani Yogatama,Fazl Barez,Michael Saxon*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出当前机器学习模型评估局限于表面性能指标的问题，主张可解释性应作为更深层次的模型评估手段，并探索其在识别根本原因、检测隐蔽故障、预测潜在问题三个方面的评估功能，同时强调可解释性方法需满足可证伪、可复现、可预测的科学标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习模型评估主要依赖行为快照式的基准准确率、胜率和结果指标，但这种表面评估无法深入理解模型产生特定行为的原因。论文认为若信任可解释性，它应能作为比表面性能指标更丰富、更原则性的模型评估形式。

Method: 探索可解释性作为模型评估的三种方式：(1) 通过识别问题根本原因来修复缺陷；(2) 检测使模型输出失效的微妙故障机制；(3) 通过全面理解模型弱点来预测潜在问题。

Result: 论证了可解释性不仅可作为诊断工具，更可成为深层次的模型评估手段；提出可解释性评估功能的三个维度；确立可解释性方法应满足的科学标准。

Conclusion: 为实现可解释性的评估潜力，必须建立科学标准：可解释性方法需产生可证伪、可复现、可预测的论断。这为可解释性研究指明了新的质量标准和发展方向。

Abstract: Current machine learning models are evaluated through behavioral snapshots, with benchmark accuracies, win rates and outcome-based metrics. Model explanations and evaluations, however, are fundamentally intertwined: understanding why a model produces a behavior can be as important as measuring what it produces. If we trusted interpretability, we argue that it can serve not merely as diagnostics but as a richer and more principled form of model evaluation beyond surface-level performance metrics. We explore three ways interpretability can function evaluatively: (1) fixing problems by identifying the root causes of unwanted behavior, (2) detecting subtly faulty mechanisms that invalidate model outputs, and (3) predicting potential issues before they arise by fully understanding the model's weaknesses. To fulfill its evaluative potential, we argue that interpretability methods must generate claims that are falsifiable, reproducible, and predictive -- that is, interpretability must meet scientific standards.

</details>


### [71] [The Missing Evaluation Axis: What 10,000 Student Submissions Reveal About AI Tutor Effectiveness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05648)
*Rose Niousha,Samantha Boatright Smith,Bita Akram,Peter Brusilovsky,Arto Hellas,Juho Leinonen,John DeNero,Narges Norouzi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究提出将AI导师评估从单纯的教学法质量扩展到行为维度（基于学生互动数据），通过对10,235份代码提交的分析，发现不同AI导师在学生参与模式上存在显著差异，且行为信号比教学法质量更能预测学生感知，证明了行为评估的重要价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI导师系统的评估主要关注反馈信息的教学法质量，忽略了关键问题：学生收到反馈后实际做了什么。单纯依赖教学法评估是不够的，需要从学生行为角度进行补充性评估。

Method: 研究者提出了一套行为评估框架，并应用于10,235份代码提交及其对应AI导师反馈的数据集，比较了不同学期间部署的两个AI导师的表现差异，测量指标包括学生是否采取行动以及行动是否正确。

Result: 两个AI导师在学生参与模式上存在显著差异，这些差异在仅基于教学法质量的评估中无法被发现。此外，学生感知反馈有用性的预测因素中，行为信号比单纯的教学法质量关联性更强。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，行为维度的评估能够补充传统的教学法评估，提供更完整、更具可操作性的AI导师性能评估视角。建议未来AI教育系统的评估应将学生互动数据纳入考量。

Abstract: Current Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based tutoring systems (AI tutors) are primarily evaluated based on the pedagogical quality of their feedback messages. While important, pedagogy alone is insufficient because it ignores a critical question: what do students actually do with the feedback they receive? We argue that AI tutor evaluation should be extended with a behavioral dimension grounded in student interaction data, which complements pedagogical assessment. We propose an evaluation framework and apply it to 10,235 code submissions with corresponding AI tutor feedback from an introductory undergraduate programming course to measure whether students act on tutor feedback and whether those actions are applied correctly. Using this framework to compare two deployed AI tutors across different semesters in a large-scale introductory computer science course reveals substantial differences in student engagement patterns that are not captured by pedagogy-only evaluation. Moreover, these engagement-based behavioral signals are more strongly associated with student perception of helpful feedback than pedagogical quality alone, providing a more complete and actionable picture of AI tutor performance.

</details>


### [72] [A Benchmark for Strategic Auditee Gaming Under Continuous Compliance Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06340)
*Florian A. D. Burnat,Brittany I. Davidson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究将持续合规审计形式化为Stackelberg博弈，揭示了静态审计设计中覆盖缺口与粒度缺口不可同时关闭的结构性限制(Observation 1)，并通过Periodic-with-floor和怀疑升级两种策略从正交方向突破这一限制，OffAuditDrift策略则展现了对两者防御的克服能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴法规(EU AI Act和Digital Services Act)要求的持续部署后合规审计创造了与以往单次输入/输出博弈截然不同的战略性博弈类别。监管系统可通过延迟结果报告、在合理噪声范围内漂移报告、利用纵向样本流失、以及在模糊指标定义间进行选择性采样等方式进行策略性博弈。

Method: 将连续审计形式化为审计者承诺时序策略与自适应被审计者之间的T轮Stackelberg博弈；识别了噪声感知静态审计设计的结构性特征——"覆盖机制"，其中覆盖缺口和粒度缺口无法同时关闭(观察1)；提出两种最小扩展策略：(1)样本量感知的静态规则(Periodic-with-floor)关闭粒度失效案例；(2)历史条件化的怀疑升级策略关闭朴素Drift策略的覆盖失效案例；设计OffAuditDrift策略利用Stackelberg承诺击败两种防御。

Result: 验证了观察1的预测：两种最小扩展策略各自沿正交轴关闭覆盖机制，但均无法同时关闭两类失败；样本量感知的Periodic-with-floor规则有效关闭粒度失败案例，历史条件化怀疑升级策略有效关闭朴素Drift策略的覆盖失败案例；OffAuditDrift策略利用Stackelberg承诺结构击败了两种防御策略。

Conclusion: 本研究贡献了一个非加性危害分解框架(W, C)，揭示了流失效应如何将危害从监管可见的表面转移到监管盲区；提供了5个审计对象策略库(Delay, Drift, Cherry-pick, Attrition, OffAuditDrift)和5个审计者策略库，基于DSA透明度数据库统计校准；并开发了具有可扩展Python接口的可复现模拟器，为连续审计的实证研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Continuous post-deployment compliance audits, mandated by emerging regulations such as the EU AI Act and Digital Services Act, create a class of strategic gaming distinct from the one-shot input/output gaming studied in prior work. Regulated systems can delay outcome reporting, drift their reports within plausible noise envelopes, exploit longitudinal sample attrition, and cherry-pick among ambiguous metric definitions. We formalize continuous auditing as a $T$-round Stackelberg game between an auditor that commits to a temporal policy and an adaptive auditee, and identify a structural feature of any noise-aware static-auditor design: a cover regime in which coverage gaps and granularity gaps cannot be closed simultaneously. We make this formal as Observation 1 and show that two minimal extension policies, each derived from the observation, close the regime along orthogonal axes: a sample-size-aware static rule (Periodic-with-floor) closes the granularity-failure case, while a history-conditioned suspicion-escalation policy closes the coverage-failure case for the naive Drift strategy -- and neither closes both, exactly as the observation predicts; an audit-aware OffAuditDrift strategy that exploits Stackelberg commitment defeats both. To support empirical study we contribute a non-additive harm decomposition (welfare loss $W$, coverage loss $C$) that exposes how attrition shifts harm from the regulator-accountable surface to a regulator-invisible one; an initial library of five auditee strategies (Delay, Drift, Cherry-pick, Attrition, OffAuditDrift) and five auditor policies, calibrated to summary statistics from published audits of the DSA Transparency Database; and a reproducible simulator with a small, extensible Python interface.

</details>

### [73] [Auto Research with Specialist Agents Develops Effective and Non-Trivial Training Recipes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05724)
*Jingjie Ning,Xiaochuan Li,Ji Zeng,Hao Kang,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个完全自主运作的自动研究系统，通过外部评估驱动的闭环实验机制实现。系统由专家智能体组成，能够基于谱系反馈将评估结果（包括崩溃、预算超支、规模失败和精度门缺失）转化为后续程序级配方编辑，无需人工干预。在1,197次主要试验和600次参数优化对照试验中，人类未参与提案选择、配方编辑、分数覆盖或失败试验修复。三项基准测试分别取得0.81%验证bpb降低、38.7% CORE提升和4.59% wallclock时间减少的成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动机器学习(AutoML)系统通常依赖人类选择提案、编辑配方或修复失败试验，限制了自动化程度和探索效率。本研究旨在构建一个完全自主的闭环研究系统，能够持续进行实验提案、代码修改、评估反馈和迭代改进。

Method: 1. 构建封闭的实验循环：每次提交包含假设、可执行代码编辑、评估者拥有的结果和塑造下一提案的反馈
2. 使用专家智能体对配方空间进行分区，并跨试验共享测量到的谱系信息
3. 核心创新：谱系反馈机制使智能体能够将评估结果（包括各种失败模式）转化为后续程序级配方编辑，而非一次性建议
4. 严格遵守外部评估器和合法性检查

Result: 在三个主要基准测试中：NanoChat-D12 CORE提升38.7%；CIFAR-10 Airbench96 wallclock减少4.59%；Parameter Golf验证bpb降低0.81%。系统包含157次提交的程序重写审计（如NanoChat注意力核路径变更）。

Conclusion: 该系统能够在无需人工干预的情况下自主编写代码、提交实验、吸收反馈、应用和组合已知技术，并改进公开的起始配方。谱系反馈是关键机制，使系统能够将失败转化为后续改进，体现了真正的自动研究能力。

Abstract: We study auto research as a closed empirical loop driven by external measurement. Each submitted trial carries a hypothesis, an executable code edit, an evaluator-owned outcome, and feedback that shapes the next proposal. The output is not a generated paper or a single model checkpoint, but an auditable trajectory of proposals, code diffs, experiments, scores, and failure labels. We instantiate this loop with specialist agents that partition recipe surfaces and share measured lineage across trials. The central empirical finding is that lineage feedback lets agents turn evaluator outcomes, including crashes, budget overruns, size failures, and accuracy-gate misses, into later program-level recipe edits rather than one-shot suggestions. Across 1,197 headline-run trials plus 600 Parameter Golf control trials after one-time setup and launch, humans did not choose proposals, edit recipes, override scores, or repair failed trials during the search. In the three headline runs, the same submitted-trial loop reduces Parameter Golf validation bpb by $0.81\%$, raises NanoChat-D12 CORE by $38.7\%$, and reduces CIFAR-10 Airbench96 wallclock by $4.59\%$, with each task measured by its own external evaluator and legality checks. The trace includes a strict architecture-domain audit of 157 headline-run submissions and program rewrites such as a NanoChat attention-kernel path change. Within this scope the loop autonomously writes code, submits experiments, absorbs feedback, applies and combines known techniques inside each environment, and improves public starting recipes.

</details>


### [74] [Multiagent Stochastic Shortest Path Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06056)
*Martin Jonáš,Antonín Kučera,Vojtěch Kůr,Jan Mačák,Vojtěch Řehák*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: The paper introduces and analyzes the multi-agent stochastic shortest path (MSSP) problem, where k agents aim to minimize expected time for any agent to reach a target. The authors study computational and strategic complexity across autonomous and coordinated settings, and propose efficient algorithms validated experimentally.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Multi-agent systems require coordination to achieve common goals efficiently. The MSSP problem formalizes minimizing expected time for any agent to reach a target state, which is a fundamental problem in multi-agent planning under uncertainty.

Method: The authors analyze the computational complexity in autonomous and coordinated settings, and design efficient algorithms for strategy synthesis.

Result: The paper provides complexity analysis and efficient algorithms for both autonomous and coordinated settings, with experimental validation showing scalability.

Conclusion: Efficient strategy-synthesis algorithms are designed and experimentally validated against natural baselines on instances of increasing size.

Abstract: We introduce and study the multi-agent stochastic shortest path (MSSP) problem, in which $k$ agents strive to reach a target state, aiming to minimize the expected time to reach the target by any agent. We analyze the computational and strategy-complexity of the problem in both autonomous and coordinated settings, and we design efficient strategy-synthesis algorithms. The algorithms are experimentally evaluated on instances of increasing size against natural baselines.

</details>


### [75] [Power-Efficiency and Scalability Analysis of Magnetically-Actuated Satellite Swarms via Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06286)
*Yuta Takahashi,Seang Shim,Hiraku Sakamoto,Shin-ichiro Sakai*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出基于凸优化的磁驱动卫星集群功耗评估框架，解决了电磁力/力矩非线性模型带来的非凸功耗约束难题，证明了增加卫星数量可提升编队保持功耗效率，为大规模空间系统的磁驱动编队方案提供理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间分布式孔径可突破发射尺寸限制，实现高增益、窄波束和大孔径能力，但其长期维护面临轨道动力学不稳定和编队控制一致性的挑战，磁驱动策略虽可实现无推进剂位置控制，但电磁模型非线性导致的非凸功耗约束使系统级配置分析困难。

Method: 建立基于凸优化的分析框架，将电磁力与力矩模型的非线性特性转化为可处理的凸优化问题，评估大规模磁驱动卫星集群的功耗特性。

Result: 增加卫星数量可提高编队保持的功耗效率，磁驱动集群架构相比传统少量卫星电磁编队飞行方案具有更好的功耗效率。

Conclusion: 磁驱动卫星集群架构为构建大规模空间系统提供了一种节能替代方案，可作为传统少量卫星电磁编队飞行概念的补充。

Abstract: This correspondence presents a convex-optimization-based evaluation framework of satellite-swarm-based apertures maintained by magnetic-field interactions. Spaceborne distributed apertures are composed of multiple satellites and are attractive for scientific and commercial missions because their scalability enables high-gain, narrow-beam, and large-aperture capabilities beyond the launch-size limitations. A key challenge is that the long-term maintenance of such virtual structures requires consistent formation control amid unstable orbital dynamics, and magnetic interactions generated by satellite-mounted magnetorquers offer a desirable propellant-free position-control strategy. However, the nonlinearities of the electromagnetic force and torque model lead to a nonconvex power-consumption constraint, making system-level configuration analysis difficult. To address this issue, we develop a convex optimization-based framework to analyze the power consumption of large magnetically actuated satellite swarms. The resulting analysis shows that increasing the number of satellites can improve formation-keeping power efficiency. This indicates that magnetically actuated swarm architectures provide a power-efficient alternative to the conventional few-satellite electromagnetic formation-flight concept for constructing large-scale space systems.

</details>


### [76] [Coordination Matters: Evaluation of Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06557)
*Maria Ana Cardei,Matthew Landers,Afsaneh Doryab*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文针对现有合作式多智能体强化学习评估仅关注结果指标而忽视协调过程的问题，提出协同感知评估视角，并构建STAT测试平台系统评估六种MARL方法。研究发现相同回报表现可能掩盖不同的协调机制，性能受分配压力和冗余选择等因素影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合作式多智能体强化学习基准测试主要关注回报率、成功率或完成时间等汇总指标，这些指标无法揭示智能体在规模化的任务分配场景中如何协调决策。

Method: 提出STAT测试平台，在保持观测访问和任务规则不变的前提下，系统性变化智能体、任务和环境规模；评估了六种具有代表性的基于值函数的多智能体强化学习方法，涵盖不同集中化程度。

Result: 相似回报趋势可反映不同的协调机制（冗余分配、分配多样性、任务完成效率存在差异）；在约束承诺型任务分配中，规模下的性能不仅受制于标称动作空间大小，还受分配压力、稀疏决策机会和相互依赖智能体的冗余选择影响。

Conclusion: 协同感知评估应作为基于回报的基准测试的必要补充，以揭示智能体间的协调机制差异。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) benchmarks commonly emphasize aggregate outcomes such as return, success rate, or completion time. While essential, these metrics often fail to reveal how agents coordinate, particularly in settings where agents, tasks, and joint assignment choices scale combinatorially. We propose a coordination-aware evaluation perspective that supplements return with process-level diagnostics. We instantiate this perspective using STAT, a controlled commitment-constrained spatial task-allocation testbed that systematically varies agents, tasks, and environment size while holding observation access and task rules fixed. We evaluate six representative value-based MARL methods across varying levels of centralization. Our results show that similar return trends can reflect distinct coordination mechanisms, including differences in redundant assignment, assignment diversity, and task-completion efficiency. We find that in commitment-constrained task allocation, performance under scale is shaped not only by nominal action-space size, but also by assignment pressure, sparse decision opportunities, and redundant choices among interdependent agents. Our findings motivate coordination-aware evaluation as a necessary complement to return-based benchmarking for cooperative MARL.

</details>

### [77] [Quantizing With Randomized Hadamard Transforms: Efficient Heuristic Now Proven](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06014)
*Ran Ben-Basat,William Kuszmaul,Michael Mitzenmacher,Amit Portnoy,Shay Vargaftik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了随机Hadamard变换替代均匀随机旋转的理论保证。证明了两个RHT可渐近匹配URR用于现代压缩方案（DRIVE、QUIC-FL），三个RHT可满足向量量化的块相关性要求，并提出运行时自适应策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: URR在量化压缩等场景中常用但计算成本高，RHT虽可高效实现但单次RHT对最坏情况输入表现不佳，需理论保证替代方案的可行性。

Method: 理论分析证明两个RHT后坐标边际分布与高斯分布的Kolmogorov距离和1-Wasserstein距离均为O(d^{-1/2})；三个RHT композиции证明坐标协方差衰减；提出运行时矩检验自适应算法。

Result: 两个RHT在渐近意义上与URR性能相当（适用于DRIVE和QUIC-FL）；三个RHT确保VQ中固定大小坐标块的弱相关性要求；动态调整策略可提升实际应用性能。

Conclusion: 两个RHT足以在渐近意义上替代URR用于DRIVE和QUIC-FL压缩方案；三个RHT可确保VQ代码簿的性能与URR相当；实践中可通过O(d)时间复杂度的矩检验动态调整RHT数量以平衡性能与效率。

Abstract: Uniform random rotations (URRs) are a common preprocessing step in modern quantization approaches used for gradient compression, inference acceleration, KV-cache compression, model weight quantization, and approximate nearest-neighbor search in vector databases. In practice, URRs are often replaced by randomized Hadamard transforms (RHTs), which preserve orthogonality while admitting fast implementations. The remaining issue is the performance for worst-case inputs. With a URR, each coordinate is individually distributed as a shifted beta distribution, which converges to a Gaussian distribution in high dimensions. Generally, one RHT is not suitable in the worst case, as individual coordinates can be far from these distributions. We show that after composing two RHTs on any $d$-sized input vector, the marginal distribution of every fixed coordinate of the normalized rotated vector is within $O(d^{-1/2})$ of a standard Gaussian both in Kolmogorov distance and in $1$-Wasserstein distance. We then plug these bounds into the analyses of modern compression schemes, namely DRIVE and QUIC-FL, and show that two RHTs achieve performance that asymptotically matches URRs.
  However, we show that two RHTs may not be sufficient for Vector Quantization (VQ), which often requires weak correlation across fixed-size blocks of coordinates (as opposed to only marginal distribution convergence for single coordinates). We prove that a composition of three RHTs leads to decaying coordinate covariance. This ensures that any fixed, bounded, multi-dimensional VQ codebook optimized for URRs has the same expected error when using three RHTs, up to an additive term that vanishes with the dimension.
  Finally, because practical inputs are rarely adversarial, we propose a linear-time ${O}(d)$ check on the input's moments to dynamically adapt the number of RHTs used at runtime to improve performance.

</details>


### [78] [Contrastive Identification and Generation in the Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06211)
*Xiaoyu Li,Andi Han,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究将极限识别与生成框架从全标注/正例数据扩展到对比数据（无序对 $\{x,y\}$ 满足 $h(x)\neq h(y)$ 但正例标签未知），引入通用交叉图作为统一技术工具，建立了可识别性精确刻画、对比闭包维度、生成-文本识别层次关系以及对有限对抗腐败的尖锐逆转等核心结果。拓展极限识别框架至对比关系数据；通用交叉图统一编码歧义、生成障碍与腐败；精确刻画可识别性条件与生成复杂度；揭示与经典模型不可比较的层次关系和对噪声的尖锐逆转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gold [1967]的经典极限识别模型和Kleinberg等 [2024]的极限生成模型均只处理正例或全标注数据，而现实中许多监督信号本质上是关系性的而非单例的，编码的是样本间的关系而非单一样本的标签，现有模型无法处理此类对比性数据。需建立对比极限识别与生成的统一理论。动机: Gold [1967] 的经典识别模型和Kleinberg & Mullainathan [2024] 的生成模型都局限于正例或全标注数据，而现实中的监督信号往往是关系性的（编码样本间关系而非单一样本标签），现有框架无法处理。

Method: 引入通用交叉图（common crossing graph）作为统一技术对象，将成对歧义、家族级生成障碍和腐败缺陷统一编码为覆盖-关联语言；给出了Angluin的tell-tale条件的单行几何细化；定义了对比闭包维度（contrastive closure dimension）。

Result: （1）精确刻画了可对比识别的类别条件（Angluin [1980] tell-tale条件的几何细化）；（2）定义了对比闭包维度并精确刻画了均匀对比生成的样本复杂度上界；（3）证明对比生成与文本识别相互不可比较；（4）证明了对有限对抗腐败的尖锐逆转：存在类别在对任何有限腐败预算下都可由单一预算无关算法从对比对中识别，但即使仅有一个腐败观测也无法从正例中识别。

Conclusion: 建立了对比学习识别与生成的完整理论框架，证明了该模型与经典正例识别和文本识别的严格层次关系，并揭示了对有限对抗扰动的尖锐逆转现象，为关系监督信号的学习理论奠定了基础。

Abstract: In the classical identification in the limit model of Gold [1967], a stream of positive examples is presented round by round, and the learner must eventually recover the target hypothesis. Recently, Kleinberg and Mullainathan [2024] introduced generation in the limit, where the learner instead must eventually output novel elements of the target's support. Both lines of work focus on positive-only or fully labeled data. Yet many natural supervision signals are inherently relational rather than singleton, which encode relationships between examples rather than labels of individual ones. We initiate the study of contrastive identification and generation in the limit, where the learner observes a contrastive presentation of data: a stream of unordered pairs $\{x,y\}$ satisfying $h(x)\ne h(y)$ for an unknown target binary hypothesis $h$, but which element is positive is hidden from the learner. We first present three results in the noiseless setting: an exact characterization of contrastive identifiable classes (a one-line geometric refinement of Angluin [1980]'s tell-tale condition), a combinatorial dimension called contrastive closure dimension (a contrasitive analogue of the closure dimension in Raman et al. [2025]) and exactly characterizing uniform contrastive generation with tight sample complexity, and a strict hierarchy in which contrastive generation and text identification are mutually incomparable. We then prove a sharp reversal under finite adversarial corruption: there exist classes identifiable from contrastive pairs under any finite corruption budget by a single budget-independent algorithm, yet not identifiable from positive examples under even one corrupted observation. The unifying technical object is the common crossing graph, which encodes pairwise ambiguity, family-level generation obstructions, and corruption defects in a single coverage-and-incidence language.

</details>


### [79] [Recursive Agent Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06639)
*Apurva Gandhi,Satyaki Chakraborty,Xiangjun Wang,Aviral Kumar,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出递归智能体优化（RAO），一种用于训练递归智能体的强化学习方法，使智能体能够在推理时通过分而治之策略实现递归扩展。实验表明，该方法可提升训练效率、突破上下文窗口限制泛化至更难任务，并减少计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体系统难以有效扩展到更长上下文和更复杂问题，缺乏一种自然利用分而治之策略进行递归推理的方法。需要训练模型学会何时以及如何委托子任务和进行通信。

Method: 提出递归智能体优化（RAO），一种强化学习方法，用于训练能够递归生成和委托子任务的递归智能体。该方法教智能体在推理时如何最优地利用递归推理，实现自然扩展。

Result: 训练的递归智能体在训练效率上更优，可扩展至超出模型上下文窗口的任务，能泛化至比训练任务难得多的任务，且相比单智能体系统可减少实际运行时间。

Conclusion: 递归智能体结合RAO训练方法能够实现更好的训练效率、更强的可扩展性和泛化能力，为构建高效、可扩展的智能体系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: We introduce Recursive Agent Optimization (RAO), a reinforcement learning approach for training recursive agents: agents that can spawn and delegate sub-tasks to new instantiations of themselves recursively. Recursive agents implement an inference-time scaling algorithm that naturally allows agents to scale to longer contexts and generalize to more difficult problems via divide-and-conquer. RAO provides a method to train models to best take advantage of such recursive inference, teaching agents when and how to delegate and communicate. We find that recursive agents trained in this way enjoy better training efficiency, can scale to tasks that go beyond the model's context window, generalize to tasks much harder than the ones the agent was trained on, and can enjoy reduced wall-clock time compared to single-agent systems.

</details>


### [80] [LLMSpace: Carbon Footprint Modeling for Large Language Model Inference on LEO Satellites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05615)
*Lei Jiang,Adrian Ildefonso,Daniel Loveless,Fan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出首个太空LLM推理碳建模框架LLMSpace，填补了AI卫星全生命周期碳足迹评估的空白，揭示了运营与嵌入碳排放的多维权衡，为太空AI系统的绿色设计提供系统性分析工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）推理产生快速增长的能源需求，导致能源和碳排放危机；虽然太阳能驱动的AI近地轨道（LEO）卫星被提出以减少地面电力消耗，但其全生命周期碳足迹由于发射排放、卫星制造和辐射加固硬件需求而未被充分理解。

Method: 提出了LLMSpace碳建模框架，首次联合建模运营碳排放和嵌入碳排放，整合外围子系统、辐射加固加速器和存储器特性，以及LLM特定工作负载特征（如prefill-decode行为和token生成），使用真实的卫星和GPU配置进行评估。

Result: LLMSpace揭示了碳足迹、推理延迟、硬件设计和运营寿命之间的关键权衡，为太空LLM推理的可持续性设计提供指导。

Conclusion: LLMSpace为可持续太空LLM推理提供了系统性的碳评估框架，揭示了碳足迹、推理延迟、硬件设计与运行寿命之间的关键权衡，为未来太空AI系统的绿色设计提供了重要参考。源代码已公开于GitHub。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) impose rapidly growing energy demands, creating an emerging energy and carbon crisis driven by large-scale inference. Solar-powered, AI-enabled low Earth orbit (LEO) satellites have been proposed to mitigate terrestrial electricity consumption, but their lifecycle carbon footprint remains poorly understood due to launch emissions, satellite manufacturing, and radiation-hardened hardware requirements. This paper presents \textit{LLMSpace}, the first carbon modeling framework for LLM inference on AI-enabled LEO satellites. LLMSpace jointly models operational and embodied carbon, peripheral subsystems, radiation-hardened accelerators and memories, and LLM-specific workload characteristics such as prefill-decode behavior and token generation. Using realistic satellite and GPU configurations, LLMSpace reveals key trade-offs among carbon footprint, inference latency, hardware design, and operational lifetime for sustainable space-based LLM inference. Source code: https://github.com/UnchartedRLab/LLMSpace.

</details>

Abstract: AI's growing compute demand and new datacenter buildouts present major capacity and reliability challenges for the electricity grid, leading to multi-year interconnection delays for new datacenters and bottlenecking AI growth. To ease this strain, datacenters increasingly offer rapid power flexibility in response to grid signals, where the datacenter can increase or decrease its power consumption by adapting its workload in real time.
  In order to understand the impact of large datacenters on the grid and to facilitate the design of effective coordination strategies, we build OpenG2G, a simulation platform for AI datacenter-grid runtime coordination. We show that OpenG2G is capable of answering a wide range of coordination questions by allowing users to implement and compare various control paradigms (including classic, optimization, and learning-based controllers), and quantify how AI model and deployment choices affect datacenter flexibility and coordination outcomes. This versatility is enabled by OpenG2G's modular and extensible architecture: a datacenter backend driven by real measurements of production-grade AI services, a grid backend built on high-fidelity grid simulators, and a generic controller interface that closes the loop between them. We describe the design of OpenG2G and demonstrate its usefulness through realistic grid scenarios and AI workloads.

</details>


### [82] [CLAD: A Clustered Label-Agnostic Federated Learning Framework for Joint Anomaly Detection and Attack Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06571)
*Iason Ofeidis,Nikos Papadis,Randeep Bhatia,Leandros Tassiulas,TV Lakshman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper proposes CLAD, a federated learning framework for IoT intrusion detection that combines Clustered Federated Learning with a dual-mode architecture to simultaneously address device heterogeneity and label scarcity. The framework achieves 30% better detection performance with 80% unlabeled data and reduces communication costs by 50%.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The rapid expansion of IoT and IIoT creates a massive and heterogeneous attack surface that challenges traditional network security mechanisms. Standard Federated Learning approaches struggle to generalize across diverse device behaviors and typically fail to utilize the vast amounts of unlabeled data present in realistic edge environments, leading to two primary bottlenecks: device heterogeneity and label scarcity.

Method: The CLAD framework combines Clustered Federated Learning (CFL) with a novel Dual-Mode Micro-Architecture (DM²A). The DM²A component features a shared encoder with two branches: one for unsupervised anomaly detection and one for supervised attack classification, enabling simultaneous utilization of labeled and unlabeled data. The clustering component dynamically groups devices with congruent traffic patterns to prevent global model divergence.

Result: CLAD achieves a 30% relative improvement in detection performance in scenarios with 80% unlabeled clients, while only requiring half the communication cost compared to baseline methods.

Conclusion: CLAD framework effectively addresses both device heterogeneity and label scarcity challenges in IoT security through its integrated CFL and DM²A architecture. The approach ensures no data is discarded while preserving distinct operational patterns, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines significantly.

Abstract: The rapid expansion of the Internet of Things (IoT) and Industrial IoT (IIoT) has created a massive, heterogeneous attack surface that challenges traditional network security mechanisms. While Federated Learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving alternative to centralized Intrusion Detection Systems (IDS), standard approaches struggle to generalize across diverse device behaviors and typically fail to utilize the vast amounts of unlabeled data present in realistic edge environments. To bridge these gaps, we propose CLAD, a holistic framework that seamlessly incorporates Clustered Federated Learning (CFL) with a novel Dual-Mode Micro-Architecture ($\text{DM}^2\text{A}$). This unified approach simultaneously tackles the two primary bottlenecks of IoT security: device heterogeneity and label scarcity. The $\text{DM}^2\text{A}$ component features a shared encoder followed by two branches, enabling joint unsupervised anomaly detection and supervised attack classification; this allows the framework to harvest intelligence from both labeled and unlabeled clients. Concurrently, the clustering component dynamically groups devices with congruent traffic patterns, preventing global model divergence. By carefully combining these elements, CLAD ensures that no data is discarded and distinct operational patterns are preserved. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that this integrated approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a 30% relative improvement in detection performance in scenarios with 80% unlabeled clients, with only half the communication cost.

</details>


### [83] [Internalizing Outcome Supervision into Process Supervision: A New Paradigm for Reinforcement Learning for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05226)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Sibo wang,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将强化学习推理重新定义为"结果监督内化为过程监督"的问题，提出通过识别、纠正和重用失败轨迹来自动提取过程级信号的方法，实现仅在结果级监督下的细粒度信用分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习推理面临结果监督信号的稀疏性问题，需要将末端反馈转化为能指导中间推理步骤的细粒度信号

Method: 监督内化方法：通过识别、纠正和重用失败推理轨迹，自动提取过程级学习信号

Result: 在结果级监督下实现更细粒度的策略优化，开辟了内部生成过程监督的新训练范式

Conclusion: 提出监督内化方法，将结果级监督自动转化为过程级学习信号，实现仅在结果监督下的细粒度策略优化

Abstract: The central challenge of reinforcement learning for reasoning lies not only in the sparsity of outcome-level supervision, but more fundamentally in how to transform feedback provided only at the end of a sequence into fine-grained learning signals that can guide intermediate reasoning steps. Existing approaches either rely on outcome-level rewards for sequence-level optimization, which makes precise credit assignment difficult, or depend on externally constructed process supervision, which is costly and difficult to scale sustainably. To address this, we propose a new perspective: reinforcement learning for reasoning can be understood as the problem of internalizing outcome supervision into process supervision. From this perspective, we introduce a supervision-internalization method for reinforcement learning for reasoning, enabling the model to automatically extract process-level learning signals through identifying, correcting, and reusing failed reasoning trajectories, thereby achieving finer-grained policy optimization under outcome-only supervision. We further abstract this idea into a new training paradigm, in which the model continually generates and refines its own internal process supervision during reinforcement learning, opening a new path for fine-grained credit assignment in reinforcement learning for reasoning that differs from externally provided process supervision.

</details>


### [84] [A Unified Benchmark for Evaluating Knowledge Graph Construction Methods and Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05476)
*Othmane Kabal,Mounira Harzallah,Fabrice Guillet,Hideaki Takeda,Ryutaro Ichise*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于评估GNN在噪声文本衍生图谱上性能以及图构建方法有效性的双用途基准测试框架。该框架基于生物医学领域语料库，包含多种自动构建图谱与专家参考图谱，通过半监督节点分类实现系统化评估，具有标准化、可重复和可扩展的特点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本自动构建的知识图谱存在噪声、碎片化和语义不一致问题，严重影响GNN在下游任务中的性能。现有方法难以区分性能瓶颈源于学习模型还是图谱本身质量，缺乏系统化的评估手段。

Method: 在生物医学领域从单一语料库构建基准测试，包含两种使用不同抽取方法自动构建的图谱，以及由专家标注的高质量参考图谱作为性能上界。通过半监督节点分类任务实现对图构建方法和GNN鲁棒性的可控比较与系统评估。

Result: 该基准测试可同时评估GNN在噪声图谱上的性能以及不同图构建方法的有效性，支持对构建方法的受控比较和对GNN鲁棒性的系统评估。

Conclusion: 该基准测试提供了一个标准化、可重复且可扩展的评估框架，能够促进新的图抽取方法和学习模型的整合，为评估文本知识图谱质量与GNN鲁棒性提供了系统化工具。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs automatically constructed from text are increasingly used in real-world applications. However, their inherent noise, fragmentation, and semantic inconsistencies significantly affect the performance of Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) on downstream tasks. Assessing their performance and robustness remains difficult, as it is often unclear whether observed results stem from the learning model or from the quality of the constructed graph itself. In this work, we introduce a dual-purpose benchmark designed to jointly evaluate (i) the performance of GNNs on noisy, text-derived graphs and (ii) the effectiveness of graph construction methods on a downstream task. The benchmark is built in the biomedical domain from a single textual corpus and includes two automatically constructed graphs generated using different extraction methods, alongside a high-quality reference graph curated by experts that serves as an upper performance bound. This design enables controlled comparison of construction methods and systematic evaluation of GNN robustness through semi-supervised node classification. We further provide a standardized, reproducible, and extensible evaluation framework, facilitating the integration of new graph extraction methods and learning models.

</details>


### [85] [Multi-Dimensional Behavioral Evaluation of Agentic Stock Prediction Systems Using LLM Judges with Closed-Loop Reinforcement Learning Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05739)
*Mohammad Al Ridhawi,Mahtab Haj Ali,Hussein Al Osman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Agentic stock prediction systems make sequences of interdependent decisions (regime detection, pathway routing, reinforcement learning control) whose individual quality is hidden by aggregate metrics such as mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) or directional accuracy. We present a behavioral evaluation framework that addresses this gap. Behavioral traces logged at every autonomous decision point are grouped into five-day episodes and scored along six domain-specific dimensions (regime detection, routing, adaptation, risk calibration, strategy coherence, error recovery) by an ensemble of three large language model (LLM) judges (GPT 5.4, Claude 4.6 Opus, Gemini 3.1 Pro). Perturbation-based validation on 420 episodes yields targeted score drops of $-1.6$ to $-2.4$ on intended dimensions versus an average of $-0.32$ on the remaining five, with cross-model agreement up to Krippendorff's $α= 0.85$. The composite behavioral score, used here only for cross-episode reporting, correlates at $ρ= 0.72$ with realized 20-day Sharpe ratio from offline backtesting. Closing the loop, the framework converts deficient per-dimension scores into a credit-assigned penalty term added to the Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) reward. Three short fine-tuning cycles, all confined to the validation period, produce on the held-out 2017-2025 test period a one-day MAPE reduction from 0.61% to 0.54% (an 11.5% relative reduction; $p<0.001$, Cohen's $d=0.31$), a directional accuracy increase from 71% to 74%, and an 18% Sharpe ratio improvement (95% bootstrap CI [8.2%, 27.4%]), with gains concentrated in high-volatility episodes where the original system was most behaviorally deficient. Results are from offline backtesting and do not address effects specific to live deployment.

</details>


### [86] [RVPO: Risk-Sensitive Alignment via Variance Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05750)
*Ivan Montero,Tomasz Jurczyk,Bhuwan Dhingra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RVPO通过惩罚奖励方差解决RLHF中的约束忽视问题，在医学推理和工具调用任务上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无批评者的RLHF方法采用算术平均聚合多目标奖励，易导致约束忽视：一个目标的高成功可能掩盖其他关键目标的失败（如安全或格式）。

Method: 提出RVPO框架，通过LogSumExp(SoftMin)算子惩罚奖励间方差，将优化目标从"最大化求和"转向"最大化一致性"。

Result: 在HealthBench上，14B模型得分提升至0.261（vs GDPO的0.215，p<0.001）；在GPQA-Diamond上保持竞争性准确率，且未出现其他多奖励方法的晚期性能下降。

Conclusion: RVPO通过方差正则化在不同模型规模下缓解了约束忽视问题，且不牺牲通用能力。

Abstract: Current critic-less RLHF methods aggregate multi-objective rewards via an arithmetic mean, leaving them vulnerable to constraint neglect: high-magnitude success in one objective can numerically offset critical failures in others (e.g., safety or formatting), masking low-performing "bottleneck" rewards vital for reliable multi-objective alignment. We propose Reward-Variance Policy Optimization (RVPO), a risk-sensitive framework that penalizes inter-reward variance during advantage aggregation, shifting the objective from "maximize sum" to "maximize consistency." We show via Taylor expansion that a LogSumExp (SoftMin) operator effectively acts as a smooth variance penalty. We evaluate RVPO on rubric-based medical and scientific reasoning with up to 17 concurrent LLM-judged reward signals (Qwen2.5-3B/7B/14B) and on tool-calling with rule-based constraints (Qwen2.5-1.5B/3B). By preventing the model from neglecting difficult constraints to exploit easier objectives, RVPO improves overall scores on HealthBench (0.261 vs. 0.215 for GDPO at 14B, $p < 0.001$) and maintains competitive accuracy on GPQA-Diamond without the late-stage degradation observed in other multi-reward methods, demonstrating that variance regularization mitigates constraint neglect across model scales without sacrificing general capabilities.

</details>


### [87] [Near-Policy: Accelerating On-Policy Distillation via Asynchronous Generation and Selective Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05940)
*Miao Rang,Zhenni Bi,Hang Zhou,Kai Han,Xuechun Wang,An Xiao,Xinghao Chen,Yunhe Wang,Hanting Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Near-Policy Distillation (NPD)方法，通过异步策略解决自回归模型知识蒸馏中的分布不匹配问题，实现8.1倍加速，并使openPangu-Embedded-1B达到68.73%的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准知识蒸馏方法在自回归模型中存在分布不匹配问题；现有on-policy方法虽能缓解该问题，但依赖计算开销大的强化学习框架，效率较低。

Method: 提出Near-Policy Distillation (NPD)异步方法，将学生模型生成与训练解耦，支持序列打包的监督微调(SFT)；集成稀疏学生更新和Δ-IFD过滤机制，通过筛选极端分布外样本来稳定优化轨迹，将更新限制在安全的近端学习区域内。

Result: 相比on-policy基线实现8.1倍加速；相比SFT提升8.09%；openPangu-Embedded-1B达到68.73%的最先进性能，优于参数量更大的Qwen3-1.7B。

Conclusion: NPD通过有效缩小后续RL的探索空间，显著提升了推理效率和最终性能，证明了异步近端蒸馏策略在平衡效率与效果方面的有效性。

Abstract: Standard knowledge distillation for autoregressive models often suffers from distribution mismatch. While on-policy methods mitigate this by leveraging student-generated outputs, they rely on computationally expensive Reinforcement Learning (RL) frameworks. To improve efficiency, we propose Near-Policy Distillation (NPD), an asynchronous approach that decouples student generation from training. This reformulation enables Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) with sequence packing. However, asynchronous updates inevitably introduce policy lag and sample noise, which can cause the behavior to drift from near-policy toward off-policy. To counteract this without sacrificing efficiency, NPD integrates sparse student updates and the $Δ$-IFD filtering mechanism, a heuristic sample selection mechanism that empirically stabilizes the optimization trajectory. By filtering extreme out-of-distribution samples, $Δ$-IFD prevents noise from dominating the gradients, ensuring updates remain within a safe proximal learning zone. Empirically, the NPD framework achieves a 8.1x speedup over on-policy baselines and outperforms SFT by 8.09%. Crucially, by effectively narrowing the exploration space for subsequent RL, our method enables openPangu-Embedded-1B to reach a state-of-the-art score of 68.73%, outperforming the substantially larger Qwen3-1.7B. Codes will be released soon.

</details>


### [88] [Is Escalation Worth It? A Decision-Theoretic Characterization of LLM Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06350)
*Dylan Bouchard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文建立了模型级联的成本-质量边界决策理论框架，证明了k模型池的边界等价于C(k,2)成对级联的点态包络，并推导出边际质量-成本相等的一阶条件。实验验证表明，成对包络已接近最优，路由器超越级联是因为规避了升级前支付便宜模型的生成成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing cascade methods treat deferral thresholds as empirical hyperparameters without theoretical guidance on the geometry of cost-quality frontiers over model pools. A principled framework is needed to understand and optimize cascade design.

Method: Decision-theoretic framework using constrained optimization and duality theory. For two-model cascades, characterized piecewise concavity of cost-quality frontier on decreasing-benefit confidence regions. For k-model pools, derived frontier as pointwise envelope over C(k,2) pairwise cascades. Established first-order conditions where single shadow price equalizes marginal quality-per-cost across stage boundaries.

Result: Validated on 5 benchmarks (MATH, MMLU, TriviaQA, SimpleQA, LiveCodeBench) across 8 models from 5 providers. Deterministic threshold cascades: full fixed chains underperform pairwise envelope; optimized subsequence cascades offer negligible held-out gains over it. Pre-generation routers exceed best cascade on 4/5 datasets by avoiding cheap model's generation cost on escalated queries.

Conclusion: Cascade performance is primarily constrained by structural cost (paying cheap model before escalation) rather than insufficient intermediate stages. Full fixed chains underperform pairwise envelopes, and optimized subsequence cascades yield minimal gains over pairwise approaches. Lightweight pre-generation routers outperform best cascade policies on 4/5 benchmarks.

Abstract: Model cascades, in which a cheap LLM defers to an expensive one on low-confidence queries, are widely used to navigate the cost-quality tradeoff at deployment. Existing approaches largely treat the deferral threshold as an empirical hyperparameter, with limited guidance on the geometry of the resulting cost-quality frontier over a model pool. We develop a decision-theoretic framework grounded in constrained optimization and duality. For a two-model cascade, we establish piecewise concavity of the cost-quality frontier on decreasing-benefit regions of the confidence support, with reciprocal shadow prices linking the budget- and quality-constrained formulations. Given a pool of $k$ models, we characterize the frontier achievable by deterministic two-model threshold cascades as the pointwise envelope over $\binom{k}{2}$ pairwise cascades, with switching points where the optimal pair changes. For $k$-model cascades, we derive first-order conditions in which a single shadow price equalizes marginal quality-per-cost across stage boundaries. We validate the framework on five benchmarks (MATH, MMLU, TriviaQA, SimpleQA, LiveCodeBench) across eight models from five providers. Within the deterministic threshold-cascade class, full fixed chains underperform the pairwise envelope, and optimized subsequence cascades do not deliver practically meaningful held-out gains over it. A lightweight pre-generation router exceeds the best cascade policy on four of five datasets, mainly because it avoids the cheap model's generation cost on queries sent directly to a larger model rather than because of a stronger routing signal. These results suggest that cascade performance is limited primarily by structural cost, since cascades pay the cheap model before any escalation decision, rather than by a shortage of intermediate stages.

</details>


### [89] [E = T*H/(O+B): A Dimensionless Control Parameter for Mixture-of-Experts Ecology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06415)
*Qingjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出单一无量纲参数E = T×H/(O+B)可预测并防止MoE模型中的死亡专家现象；E ≥ 0.5足以确保视觉和语言任务中专家生态的健康运转，有望成为MoE训练的统一诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)模型容易出现"死亡专家"现象，即某些专家永远得不到激活，导致专家生态崩溃。现有方法依赖手工设计的辅助损失(如负载均衡损失)来缓解此问题，但缺乏统一、理论驱动的预测框架。

Method: 提出无量纲控制参数E = T×H/(O+B)，整合路由温度(T)、路由熵权重(H)、oracle权重(O)和平衡权重(B)四个超参数。通过12组受控实验(8组视觉任务、4组语言任务)，累计超过11000个训练轮次，在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、TinyImageNet-200、WikiText-2和WikiText-103数据集上进行跨模态验证。

Result: E ≥ 0.5即可保证零死亡专家，无需额外的负载均衡辅助损失。六项额外发现：(1)死亡专家可被"复苏"——由平衡损失驱动路由器重新探索；(2)"正交毒性"是数据集依赖的，非普遍现象；(3)任务复杂度影响临界E阈值；(4)模型过拟合与专家生态健康解耦；(5)三层MoE自发坍缩为两层功能结构；(6)生态结构在50倍温度范围内保持温度不变性。

Conclusion: E可作为MoE训练的统一诊断工具，其意义堪比流体力学中的雷诺数。该参数通过四个超参数的简单组合，为MoE系统的专家生态健康提供了可预测的量化指标，有望取代繁琐的手工辅助损失设计。

Abstract: We introduce E = T*H/(O+B), a dimensionless control parameter that predicts whether Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models will develop a healthy expert ecology or collapse into dead experts. E combines four hyperparameters -- routing temperature T, routing entropy weight H, oracle weight O, and balance weight B -- into a single quantity. Through 12 controlled experiments (8 vision, 4 language) totaling over 11,000 training epochs, we establish that E >= 0.5 alone is sufficient to guarantee zero dead experts, removing the necessity for handcrafted load-balancing auxiliary losses. We validate this cross-modally on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet-200, WikiText-2, and WikiText-103. Six additional findings emerge: (1) dead experts can resuscitate -- triggered by balance loss driving router re-exploration; (2) ortho toxicity is dataset-dependent, not universal; (3) task complexity shifts the critical E threshold; (4) model overfitting is decoupled from expert ecological health; (5) three-tier MoE spontaneously collapses into a two-tier functional structure; (6) ecological structure is temperature-invariant across a 50x range. We propose that E serves as a unified diagnostic for MoE training, analogous to the Reynolds number in fluid dynamics.

</details>


### [90] [Invariant Features in Language Models: Geometric Characterization and Model Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06458)
*Agnibh Dasgupta,Abdullah Tanvir,Xin Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出语言模型中语义等价输入在潜在空间呈局部几何结构分布，其中语义不变性存在于不变子空间，而 paraphrase 变异沿干扰方向分布。基于此框架，本文通过几何表征、对比子空间发现方法和零样本模型归因三个贡献，验证了语义不变性可被视为潜在表示的局部几何属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型对 paraphrase 具有强鲁棒性，表明语义信息可能通过稳定的内部表示编码，但这种不变性的结构和起源尚不清晰。

Method: 提出局部几何框架，将语义等价输入映射为潜在空间中的结构化区域；开发对比子空间发现方法以分离语义变化与语义保持的变异；在零样本模型归因任务中应用不变表示。

Result: 不变结构出现在特定深度区域；语义位移主要位于干扰子空间之外；表示层面的干预表明不变成分对模型输出有因果作用；不变表示能够捕捉模型特定的几何模式，实现精确归因。

Conclusion: 语义不变性可被视为潜在表示的局部几何属性，为理解语言模型如何组织意义提供了原则性视角。

Abstract: Language models exhibit strong robustness to paraphrasing, suggesting that semantic information may be encoded through stable internal representations, yet the structure and origin of such invariance remain unclear. We propose a local geometric framework in which semantically equivalent inputs occupy structured regions in latent space, with paraphrastic variation along nuisance directions and semantic identity preserved in invariant subspaces. Building on this view, we make three contributions: (1) a geometric characterization of invariant latent features, (2) a contrastive subspace discovery method that separates semantic-changing from semantic-preserving variation, and (3) an application of invariant representations to zero-shot model attribution. Across models and layers, empirical results support these contributions. Invariant structure emerges in specific depth regions, semantic displacement lies largely outside the nuisance subspace, and representation-level interventions indicate a causal role of invariant components in model outputs. Invariant representations also capture model-specific geometric patterns, enabling accurate attribution. These findings suggest that semantic invariance can be viewed as a local geometric property of latent representations, offering a principled perspective on how language models organize meaning.

</details>


### [91] [PairAlign: A Framework for Sequence Tokenization via Self-Alignment with Applications to Audio Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06582)
*Adhiraj Banerjee,Vipul Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PairAlign proposes sequence-level self-alignment for compact audio tokenization, reformulating tokenization as conditional sequence generation with cross-view consistency training, achieving 55% token reduction while preserving edit-distance search.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing audio tokenizers rely on local quantization, clustering, or codec reconstruction, leading to poor sequence consistency, uncontrolled length, unbounded edit distances, and degenerate token distributions. These limitations prevent efficient symbolic operations like retrieval and memory.

Method: PairAlign employs conditional sequence generation with an encoder-decoder architecture. It uses cross-paired teacher forcing, EMA-teacher targets, prefix corruption, likelihood contrast, and length control to refine VQ-style tokenization. Training objectives maximize cross-view likelihood consistency between two content-preserving views while penalizing many-to-one collapse.

Result: On 3-second speech, PairAlign achieves compact, non-degenerate sequences with broad vocabulary usage. On TIMIT, it reduces archive token count by 55% while preserving edit-distance search. The probe confirms strong length control and bounded edit trajectories under 100ms shifts.

Conclusion: PairAlign presents a novel paradigm for audio tokenization by unifying representation learning with discrete sequence generation, eliminating the need for direct supervision while achieving strong edit-distance preservation and token efficiency.

Abstract: Many operations on sensory data -- comparison, memory, retrieval, and reasoning -- are naturally expressed over discrete symbolic structures. In language this interface is given by tokens; in audio, it must be learned. Existing audio tokenizers rely on quantization, clustering, or codec reconstruction, assigning tokens locally, so sequence consistency, compactness, length control, termination, and edit similarity are rarely optimized directly.
  We introduce PairAlign, a framework for compact audio tokenization through sequence-level self-alignment. PairAlign treats tokenization as conditional sequence generation: an encoder maps speech to a continuous condition, and an autoregressive decoder generates tokens from BOS, learning token identity, order, length, and EOS placement. Given two content-preserving views, each view's sequence is trained to be likely under the other's representation, while unrelated examples provide competing sequences. This gives a scalable surrogate for edit-distance preservation while discouraging many-to-one collapse.
  PairAlign starts from VQ-style tokenization and refines it with EMA-teacher targets, cross-paired teacher forcing, prefix corruption, likelihood contrast, and length control.
  On 3-second speech, PairAlign learns compact, non-degenerate sequences with broad vocabulary usage and strong cross-view consistency. On TIMIT retrieval, it preserves edit-distance search while reducing archive token count by 55%. A continuous-sweep probe shows lower local overlap than a dense geometric tokenizer, but stronger length control and bounded edit trajectories under 100 ms shifts. PairAlign is a sequence-symbolic predictive learner: like JEPA-style objectives, it predicts an abstract target from another view as a learned variable-length symbolic sequence, not a continuous latent.

</details>


### [92] [When and Why SignSGD Outperforms SGD: A Theoretical Study Based on $\ell_1$-norm Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06615)
*Hongyi Tao,Dingzhi Yu,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在理论上证明，在特定问题几何下（如ℓ₁范数平稳性、ℓ∞-平滑性和可分离噪声模型），SignSGD相比SGD具有d倍的复杂度优势，并将该理论推广至矩阵域的Muon优化器，通过124M参数GPT-2预训练验证了理论预测的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决sign-based优化算法（如SignSGD、Muon）在训练大模型时表现优异，但缺乏理论解释的核心问题。标准理论下SGD已是minimax最优，无法解释sign方法的优越性。

Method: 引入新的问题几何框架：ℓ₁范数平稳性、ℓ∞-平滑性假设，以及可分离噪声模型（更适合刻画signed更新的坐标特性）；推导SignSGD的匹配上下界；将框架推广至矩阵域分析Muon优化器。

Result: 1. 在稀疏噪声条件下，SignSGD相比SGD复杂度降低d倍（d为问题维度）；2. 将理论扩展至矩阵域，证明Muon保持此最优维度缩放特性；3. 124M参数GPT-2预训练实验验证了理论预测的收敛速度优势。

Conclusion: Sign-based优化器的优势源于其隐式地在不同问题几何下工作；本研究为理解大模型训练中sign优化器的有效性提供了理论依据，并桥接了理论与实践的gap。

Abstract: Sign-based optimization algorithms, such as SignSGD and Muon, have garnered significant attention for their remarkable performance in training large foundation models. Despite this empirical success, we still lack a theoretical understanding of when and why these sign-based methods outperform vanilla SGD. The core obstacle is that under standard smoothness and finite variance conditions, SGD is known to be minimax optimal for finding stationary points measured by $\ell_2$-norms, thereby fundamentally precluding any complexity gains for sign-based methods in standard settings. To overcome this barrier, we analyze sign-based optimizers leveraging $\ell_1$-norm stationarity, $\ell_\infty$-smoothness, and a separable noise model, which can better capture the coordinate-wise nature of signed updates. Under this distinct problem geometry, we derive matched upper and lower bounds for SignSGD and explicitly characterize the problem class in which SignSGD provably dominates SGD. Specifically, we compare the \emph{upper bound of SignSGD} with the \emph{lower bound of SGD}, illustrating that SignSGD effectively reduces the complexity by a factor of $d$ under \emph{sparse noise}, where $d$ is the problem dimension. Furthermore, we elevate this framework to the matrix domain, providing an equivalent optimal lower bound for the Muon optimizer, proving that extending the sign operator to matrices preserves this optimal scaling with dimensionality. Finally, we bridge our theoretical bounds to practice, demonstrating that the theoretical superiority of SignSGD accurately predicts its faster convergence during the pretraining of a 124M parameter GPT-2 model.

</details>


### [93] [When No Benchmark Exists: Validating Comparative LLM Safety Scoring Without Ground-Truth Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06652)
*Sushant Gautam,Finn Schwall,Annika Willoch Olstad,Fernando Vallecillos Ruiz,Birk Torpmann-Hagen,Sunniva Maria Stordal Bjørklund,Leon Moonen,Klas Pettersen,Michael A. Riegler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"无基准比较安全评分"框架，用于在缺乏标注基准的情况下评估语言模型安全性。通过"工具有效性链"替代真值标注，在SimpleAudit工具上验证可行，并在挪威公共采购案例中演示应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部署常需比较语言模型安全性，但针对特定语言、行业或监管场景的标注基准往往不存在，导致缺乏可靠的安全评估方法。

Method: (1) 形式化定义"无基准比较安全评分"问题；(2) 建立基于场景审计的评估契约；(3) 用"工具有效性链"替代ground-truth协议，包含：受控安全vs消融对比响应、目标驱动方差主导审计员/评判者伪影、跨重运行稳定性；(4) 开发SimpleAudit本地优先评分工具。

Result: Safe与abliterated目标分离AUROC达0.89-1.00；目标身份是主要方差成分(η²≈0.52)；严重性画像在10次重运行后稳定。将同一方法链应用于Petri表明其同样适用。挪威公共采购案例比较Borealis与Gemma 3显示：更安全的模型取决于场景类别和风险度量。

Conclusion: 评分结果需同时报告原始分数、匹配差值、临界率、不确定性及审计员/评判者身份，而非简化为单一排名。

Abstract: Many deployments must compare candidate language models for safety before a labeled benchmark exists for the relevant language, sector, or regulatory regime. We formalize this setting as benchmarkless comparative safety scoring and specify the contract under which a scenario-based audit can be interpreted as deployment evidence. Scores are valid only under a fixed scenario pack, rubric, auditor, judge, sampling configuration, and rerun budget. Because no labels are available, we replace ground-truth agreement with an instrumental-validity chain: responsiveness to a controlled safe-versus-abliterated contrast, dominance of target-driven variance over auditor and judge artifacts, and stability across reruns.
  We instantiate the chain in SimpleAudit, a local-first scoring instrument, and validate it on a Norwegian safety pack. Safe and abliterated targets separate with AUROC values between 0.89 and 1.00, target identity is the dominant variance component ($η^2 \approx 0.52$), and severity profiles stabilize by ten reruns. Applying the same chain to Petri shows that it admits both tools. The substantial differences arise upstream of the chain, in claim-contract enforcement and deployment fit. A Norwegian public-sector procurement case comparing Borealis and Gemma 3 demonstrates the resulting evidence in practice: the safer model depends on scenario category and risk measure. Consequently, scores, matched deltas, critical rates, uncertainty, and the auditor and judge used must be reported together rather than collapsed into a single ranking.

</details>


### [94] [Verifier-Backed Hard Problem Generation for Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06660)
*Yuhang Lai,Jiazhan Feng,Yee Whye Teh,Ning Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VHG框架，通过在 setter-solver 双角色中引入独立 verifier 形成三角色自博弈，解决大模型生成问题时 reward hacking 导致的无效性问题，在积分和数学推理任务上显著超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: While LLMs excel at solving scientific and mathematical problems, they struggle to generate valid, challenging, and novel problems. Existing approaches either rely on expensive human experts or suffer from reward hacking in naive self-play paradigms, frequently producing invalid problems.

Method: VHG proposes a three-party self-play framework integrating an independent verifier into the setter-solver duality. The verifier evaluates problem validity while the solver assesses difficulty, constraining the setter's reward jointly. Two verifier variants are instantiated: a Hard symbolic verifier and a Soft LLM-based verifier.

Result: VHG substantially outperforms all baseline methods by a clear margin on both indefinite integral tasks and general mathematical reasoning tasks.

Conclusion: VHG framework effectively addresses the reward hacking problem in self-play problem generation through verifier integration, substantially outperforming all baseline methods on indefinite integral and mathematical reasoning tasks.

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate strong capabilities for solving scientific and mathematical problems, yet they struggle to produce valid, challenging, and novel problems - an essential component for advancing LLM training and enabling autonomous scientific research. Existing problem generation approaches either depend on expensive human expert involvement or adopt naive self-play paradigms, which frequently yield invalid problems due to reward hacking. This work introduces VHG, a verifier-enhanced hard problem generation framework built upon three-party self-play. By integrating an independent verifier into the conventional setter-solver duality, our design constrains the setter's reward to be jointly determined by problem validity (evaluated by the verifier) and difficulty (assessed by the solver). We instantiate two verifier variants: a Hard symbolic verifier and a Soft LLM-based verifier, with evaluations conducted on indefinite integral tasks and general mathematical reasoning tasks. Experimental results show that VHG substantially outperforms all baseline methods by a clear margin.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [DADL: A Declarative Description Language for Enterprise Tool Libraries in LLM Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05247)
*Axel Dunkel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DADL (Dunkel API Description Language) 是一种解决大规模企业场景中 Model Context Protocol (MCP) 架构问题的方案。通过 YAML 声明式格式统一描述 REST API 的端点、认证、分页、响应格式和访问分类，在运行时解释执行而非为每个 API 部署独立服务器。这实现了凭证集中管理并将 LLM 的工具目录接口大小固定化，将上下文开销降低 142 倍，支持企业构建版本化、可审计的工具库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) has two structural problems at organizational scale: (1) Every API integration requires a dedicated server process with its own deployment, dependencies, and credential handling, with most servers being thin REST API wrappers; (2) The per-tool registration model causes context window consumption to grow linearly with API catalog size, forcing real deployments to expose only a small fraction of available APIs.

Method: DADL is a YAML-based declarative format describing REST API endpoints, authentication, pagination, response shaping, and access classification in a single file. It uses a runtime interpreter model where no per-API server process is deployed; instead, a unified runtime handles all tool definitions. This shared runtime enables centralized credential/authorization management and exposes the tool catalog to the LLM through a fixed-size Code Mode interface, breaking the linear context growth problem.

Result: DADL v0.1 specification is released under CC BY-SA 4.0 with a public registry containing 1,833 tool definitions across 20 services. Code Mode reduces tool advertisement context cost from approximately 142,000 tokens to approximately 1,000 tokens (142x reduction). Per-call search and execute costs are additional and task-dependent.

Conclusion: DADL enables an "Enterprise Tool Library" - a versioned, auditable collection of API integrations that any team can extend, share, and consume through a single authentication and authorization boundary. The approach eliminates per-API server processes and code generation while centralizing credential management.

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) is the standard interface between large language model (LLM) agents and external tools. At organizational scale, however, it exposes two structural problems. First, every API integration is shipped as a dedicated server process with its own deployment, dependency tree, and credential handling; recent empirical work shows the overwhelming majority of these servers are thin wrappers around REST APIs. Second, the per-tool registration model causes context window consumption to grow linearly with catalog size, forcing real deployments to expose only a small fraction of the APIs an organization actually uses.
  We present DADL (Dunkel API Description Language), a YAML format describing a REST API's endpoints, authentication, pagination, response shaping, and access classification in a single declarative file. A DADL file is interpreted by an execution layer at runtime; no per-API server process is deployed and no integration code is generated, though the runtime is itself a server. Because all tools share that runtime, credentials and authorization are managed centrally, and the catalog reaches the LLM through a fixed-size Code Mode interface independent of size.
