The result is an Enterprise Tool Library: a versioned, auditable collection of API integrations any team can extend, share, and consume through one authentication and authorization boundary.
  The DADL v0.1 specification is released under CC BY-SA 4.0, and a public registry contains 1,833 tool definitions across 20 services. On this catalog, Code Mode reduces the context cost of tool advertisement from approximately 142,000 tokens to approximately 1,000, a 142x reduction; the per-call cost of search and execute invocations is additional and depends on the task.

</details>


### [96] [Automated Population-Level Audit Assurance via AI-Based Document Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05252)
*Santosh Vasudevan,Velu Natarajan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于AI文档智能的大规模审计交易测试自动化框架，利用Snowflake Document AI从非结构化PDF报表中提取结构化数据，并与权威数据源进行比对核查，实现了对数百万笔交易的全面覆盖测试，取代传统的抽样审查方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的人工抽样审查方式在处理大规模交易时存在效率低、覆盖范围有限、无法实现实时风险识别等问题，无法满足连续审计的需求。审计工作需要在保证准确性的同时提升可扩展性。

Method: 1) 使用Snowflake Document AI构建文档智能模型；2) 利用约20份标注文档构建小样本学习模型；3) 从非结构化PDF报表中提取结构化数据；4) 与权威数据源进行对账核查以识别差异；5) 通过交互式仪表盘和自动化报告呈现结果。

Result: 该框架实现了总体层面（population-level）的审计测试而非抽样测试，显著提升了审计覆盖率，支持连续审计目标，具备可扩展性和近实时风险识别能力。

Conclusion: 文档智能与数据分析驱动的审计框架相结合，能够实现大规模、可扩展、近实时的风险识别和连续审计支持，为审计业务的数字化转型提供了技术可行路径。

Abstract: Audit transaction testing validates accuracy and completeness of customer-facing statements against internal systems of record. Traditional manual, sample-based review of unstructured PDF statements is labor-intensive and does not scale to millions of transactions. This paper presents an automated framework for large-scale audit transaction testing using AI-based document intelligence. The solution leverages Snowflake Document AI to extract structured data from unstructured PDF statements using a small labeled corpus (approximately 20 documents). Extracted data are reconciled against authoritative source-of-truth datasets to identify discrepancies at scale. Results are surfaced through interactive dashboards and automated reports. The framework enables population-level testing rather than sampling-based approaches, improving audit coverage and supporting continuous assurance objectives. Recent advances in document intelligence and analytics-driven audit frameworks enable scalable, near real-time risk identification and continuous assurance.

</details>


### [97] [Bridging Generation and Training: A Systematic Review of Quality Issues in LLMs for Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05267)
*Kaifeng He,Xiaojun Zhang,Peiliang Cai,Mingwei Liu,Yanlin Wang,Chong Wang,Kaifeng Huang,Bihuan Chen,Xin Peng,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文是一项系统文献综述研究，通过分析114篇主要论文，探究了训练数据质量问题如何传播并影响LLM代码生成质量。研究建立了统一分类体系，形式化了18种传播机制，并发现质量保证正从被动过滤转向主动数据治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在代码生成任务中频繁产生有缺陷的输出（逻辑错误、安全漏洞等），这些生成失败虽常被视为模型层面的局限性，但越来越多的经验证据将其根本原因追溯至训练语料库的不完善。训练数据质量问题如何传播到生成代码质量的具体机制尚不清楚。

Method: 对114篇主要研究进行系统文献综述，建立统一分类体系，将代码质量问题分为9个维度，训练数据质量问题分为代码和非代码属性，并形式化18种典型传播映射机制。

Result: 构建了统一分类体系，涵盖9个维度的代码质量问题与训练数据的代码/非代码属性；形式化了18种典型传播映射机制；综合分析了数据、模型和生成生命周期中的检测与缓解技术。文献揭示了明显的方法论转变：从被动的、基于启发式的生成后过滤，转向主动的、数据驱动的治理和闭环修复。

Conclusion: 该研究识别了开放性挑战，并勾勒出通过数据整合治理和持续评估开发可靠代码生成LLM的研究方向。论文仓库：https://github.com/SYSUSELab/From-Data-to-Code

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently generate defective outputs in code generation tasks, ranging from logical bugs to security vulnerabilities. While these generation failures are often treated as model-level limitations, empirical evidence increasingly traces their root causes to imperfections within the training corpora. Yet, the specific mechanisms linking training data quality issues to generated code quality issues remain largely unmapped. This paper presents a systematic literature review of 114 primary studies to investigate how training data quality issues propagate into code generation. We establish a unified taxonomy that categorizes generated code quality issues across nine dimensions and training data quality issues into code and non-code attributes. Based on this taxonomy, we formalize a causal framework detailing 18 typical propagation mapping mechanisms. Furthermore, we synthesize state-of-the-art detection and mitigation techniques across the data, model, and generation lifecycles. The reviewed literature reveals a clear methodological shift: quality assurance is transitioning from reactive, heuristic-based post-generation filtering toward proactive, data-centric governance and closed-loop repair. Finally, we identify open challenges and outline research directions for developing reliable LLMs for code through integrated data curation and continuous evaluation. Our repository is available at https://github.com/SYSUSELab/From-Data-to-Code.

</details>


### [98] [Mise en Place for Agentic Coding: Deliberate Preparation as Context Engineering Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05400)
*Andrew Zigler*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对AI编码智能体带来的"氛围编程"工作模式提出系统性对齐问题，借鉴烹饪概念"mise en place"提出三阶段准备方法论（情境扎根→协作式规格说明→任务分解），通过黑客松实验验证方法有效性，并引入"上下文流利度"概念连接至逆向设计和隐性知识外化理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码智能体的快速普及产生了主导工作模式"氛围编程"（vibe coding），该模式优先考虑实现速度而非深思熟虑的准备；缺乏足够上下文的智能体会产生需要大量调试和重构的代码，造成substantial开发时间消耗，形成系统性对齐问题

Method: 提出三阶段准备方法论（MEP）：(1) 情境扎根——将领域专业知识与隐性知识外化为结构化文档；(2) 协作式规格说明——通过人-智能体对话生成详细设计工件；(3) 任务分解——将规格说明转换为结构化、依赖感知的任务记录

Result: 在竞赛性黑客松中验证：约两小时准备使并发AI智能体能够快速并行实现全栈教育平台；引入"上下文流利度"（context fluency）概念作为新兴开发者技能

Conclusion: 提出研究议程：实证验证AI辅助软件开发中准备阶段方法论的有效性；将"context fluency"概念与逆向设计（backward design）和隐性知识外化框架相关联

Abstract: The rapid adoption of AI coding agents has produced a dominant workflow pattern -- often called "vibe coding" -- that prioritizes speed of implementation over deliberate preparation. We argue that this approach creates a systematic alignment problem: agents that lack sufficient context produce code requiring extensive debugging and refactoring, consuming substantial development time. Drawing on the culinary concept of mise en place (everything in its place; abbreviated MEP), we propose a three-phase preparation methodology for agentic coding: (1) contextual grounding, where domain expertise and tacit knowledge are externalized into structured documents; (2) collaborative specification, where human-agent dialogue produces detailed design artifacts; and (3) task decomposition, where specifications are converted into structured, dependency-aware task records. We report on the application of MEP during a competitive hackathon, where roughly two hours of preparation enabled a rapid parallel implementation of a full-stack educational platform by concurrent AI agents. We introduce the concept of context fluency as an emerging developer skill -- the ability to create rich, structured context that agents can act on -- and connect it to established frameworks in backward design and tacit knowledge externalization. We conclude with a research agenda for empirically validating preparation-phase methodologies in AI-assisted software development.

</details>


### [99] [Is this Build Failure Related to my Patch? An Empirical Study of Unrelated Build Failures in Continuous Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05564)
*Andie Huang,Daniel Alencar da Costa,Grant Dick,Mariam El Mezouar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究连续集成（CI）系统中无关构建失败问题，采用PU学习模型从7个Apache项目的77,354次构建失败中预测无关失败，实现precision 0.70-0.88、AUC 0.63-0.97，CI延迟、重复错误信息和前置评论数为重要预测特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CI系统中并发构建导致无关失败浪费开发者时间（平均4小时）识别失败原因，需要自动化方法区分相关与无关失败。

Method: 分析371个确认的无关失败样本，提取33个特征（来自issue报告、评论和提交），构建半监督PU学习模型。

Result: 模型在7个项目中达到precision 0.70-0.88、recall 0.30-1.00、F1 0.44-0.91、AUC 0.63-0.97；CI延迟、重复错误信息和前置评论数为关键特征；无关测试失败占20%。

Conclusion: PU学习可有效帮助开发者识别不太可能由当前推送引起的构建失败，提高开发效率。

Abstract: Continuous Integration (CI) systems often run many builds concurrently. In this setting, a legitimate build failure may not be caused by the code push that triggered it. Such unrelated build failures can waste developer effort because developers must determine whether the failure is actionable for their current change. We study 77,354 CI build failures from seven open source Apache projects to understand and predict unrelated build failures. We find that developers spend a median of 4 hours identifying whether a failure is related or unrelated to their push. We also perform a document analysis of 371 confirmed unrelated build failures sampled from 10,316 potentially unrelated failures. The analysis shows that unrelated test failures account for 20% of the cases in which developers classify build failures as unrelated. To predict unrelated build failures, we extract 33 features from issue reports, issue comments, and commits associated with the triggering push. We build semi-supervised Positive and Unlabeled (PU) learning models for seven Apache projects. The models achieve precision from 0.70 to 0.88, recall from 0.30 to 1.00, F1-score from 0.44 to 0.91, and AUC from 0.63 to 0.97. Feature importance analysis shows that CI latency, repeated error messages, and the number of preceding comments are useful indicators of unrelated build failures. These results show that PU learning can help developers identify build failures that are unlikely to be caused by their current push.

</details>


### [100] [An Empirical Study of Proactive Coding Assistants in Real-World Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05700)
*Lehui Li,Ruixuan Jia,Guo-Ye Yang,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证研究揭示了LLM模拟的IDE轨迹与真实开发者行为之间存在显著差距，并提出ProCodeBench基准数据集，表明基于模拟数据的评估可能高估真实世界性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主动式编程助手研究受限于真实开发者行为数据稀缺，不得不依赖LLM模拟的IDE轨迹，但这些模拟数据与真实开发行为的保真度尚不明确，亟需系统性的对比研究。

Method: 研究团队开发了自定义VS Code扩展，从1,246名行业开发者处收集了连续三天的真实IDE交互轨迹，并与LLM模拟的轨迹进行配对对比，从行为多样性、时间结构和探索模式三个维度进行分析。

Result: 模拟轨迹与真实轨迹在行为多样性、时间结构和探索模式方面存在显著差异；当前方法在真实IDE轨迹上的表现远不如模拟轨迹上的表现，说明基于模拟数据的评估会高估真实世界性能。

Conclusion: 模拟数据无法替代真实数据，但可在真实数据微调前作为补充；真实开发者行为数据对主动式编程助手的评估和训练至关重要。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based coding assistants have made substantial progress, yet most systems remain reactive, requiring developers to explicitly formulate their needs. Proactive coding assistants aim to infer latent developer intent from integrated development environment (IDE) interactions and repository context, thereby reducing interaction overhead and supporting more seamless assistance. However, research in this direction is limited by the scarcity of large-scale real-world developer behavior data. Existing studies therefore often rely on LLM-simulated IDE traces, whose fidelity to real development behavior remains unclear. In this paper, we investigate this simulation-to-reality gap through a large-scale empirical study. We collect real IDE interaction traces from 1{,}246 experienced industry developers over three consecutive days using a custom Visual Studio Code extension, and construct paired LLM-simulated traces for controlled comparison. Our analysis shows that simulated traces differ substantially from real traces in behavioral diversity, temporal structure, and exploratory patterns. Based on the collected data, we introduce \textbf{ProCodeBench}, a real-world benchmark for proactive intent prediction. Experiments with representative LLMs, retrieval-augmented methods, and agentic baselines show that current approaches remain far from reliable under real IDE traces, suggesting that simulation-based evaluation can overestimate real-world performance. Finally, our training study shows that simulated data cannot replace real data, but can complement it when used before real-world fine-tuning. These findings highlight the importance of real developer behavior data for evaluating and training proactive coding assistants.

</details>


### [101] [From Chat to Interview: Agentic Requirements Elicitation with an Experience Ontology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05828)
*Dongming Jin,Zhi Jin,Yaotian Yang,Linyu Li,Zheng Fang,Yuanpeng He,Wenchun Jing,Xiaohong Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出OntoAgent，一个基于经验本体引导的需求获取访谈智能体。该方法通过构建经验本体组织需求关注点，在访谈中执行四步操作识别相关需求，从而提高需求获取的有效性和问题效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求获取访谈在需求工程中至关重要但极度依赖分析师经验。现有LLM方法仅进行自由聊天，未考虑访谈和开发经验，导致遗漏隐式需求和问题冗余。

Method: OntoAgent自动分析领域需求描述构建经验本体；在访谈中执行四步操作（ParseUser、ScoreOnto、ReRankOnto、GatePrune）识别相关需求关注点；结合对话上下文生成引导性问题。

Result: 在网站应用领域的实验中，OntoAgent在需求获取有效性（IRE）上提升33%，问题效率（TKQR）上提升21%，均显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: OntoAgent通过经验本体引导实现系统化、可解释的需求获取访谈，定量和定性实验均验证其有效性，可扩展至其他领域的需求访谈任务。

Abstract: Requirements elicitation interviews are crucial and time-consuming in requirements engineering, but heavily rely on the experience of requirements analysts. Although recent advancements in large language models (LLMs) have created new opportunities to automate this process, existing approaches rely solely on LLMs for free-form chat without taking into account the interview and development experience. That leads to the omission of implicit requirements and redundant questions. Practically, experienced analysts implicitly follow a structured cognitive framework when conducting requirements elicitation. Inspired by this observation, this paper proposes an interview agent named OntoAgent for the elicitation of requirements guided by an experience ontology. OntoAgent automatically analyzes domain-specific requirements descriptions to construct an experience ontology, which organizes requirements concerns into an ontology to support systematic and explainable interviews. During the interview, OntoAgent first performs four operations (i.e., ParseUser, ScoreOnto, ReRankOnto, GatePrune) guided by the ontology to identify the relevant requirement concerns. The selected concern is then combined with the current dialogue context to generate the elicitation question. To validate OntoAgent, we conduct comprehensive quantitative experiments using the widely adopted website application domain. The results show that OntoAgent significantly outperforms existing baselines in both elicitation effectiveness and questioning efficiency, achieving a 33% improvement in IRE and a 21% improvement in TKQR. Ablation studies further validate the contribution of each key design component. In addition, a qualitative user study demonstrates its practical advantages in real-world scenarios. We believe that OntoAgent can also be extended to requirements interview tasks in other domains.

</details>


### [102] [On Fixing Insecure AI-Generated Code through Model Fine-Tuning and Prompting Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05867)
*Ali Soltanian Fard Jahromi,Amjed Tahir,Peng Liang,Foutse Khomh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: This paper systematically investigates strategies for hardening AI-generated code against Common Weakness Enumerations (CWE). Through evaluation across multiple models and programming languages using fine-tuning and prompting approaches, the authors find that security improvements are highly context-dependent with no universally effective solution. Remediation of one weakness frequently introduces new weaknesses elsewhere in the code.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI-generated code frequently contains bugs and security weaknesses, undermining trustworthiness despite strong functional performance. Existing mitigation approaches show widely varying effectiveness across models and settings.

Method: Systematic evaluation using fine-tuning and prompting approaches for model output refinement, assessing security improvements across multiple models and programming languages.

Result: Security improvements are highly strategy- and model-dependent. While some approaches reduce specific weakness classes, they often introduce new weaknesses as side effects. No single strategy consistently eliminates all weaknesses across all models and scenarios.

Conclusion: No universally effective solution exists for securing AI-generated code; different strategies work better for different models and scenarios, and remediation often introduces new weaknesses as side effects.

Abstract: The security of AI-generated code remains a major obstacle to its widespread adoption. Although code generation models achieve strong performance on functional benchmarks, their outputs frequently contain bugs and security weaknesses that undermine their trustworthiness. Prior work has explored a range of approaches to mitigate security issues in AI-generated code, e.g., using static analysis-guided generation and prompt engineering. However, their effectiveness varies widely across models and settings. This paper presents a systematic investigation of strategies for hardening model-generated code against a list of Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE). We assess the extent to which these strategies improve security across models and programming languages, using fine-tuning and prompting approaches for model output refinement. Beyond the prevalence of security weaknesses, we analyse the severity of identified CWEs, their co-occurrence, and the unintended consequences of remediation (i.e., whether fixing certain weaknesses introduces new weaknesses elsewhere in the same code). Our results show that security improvements are highly strategy- and model-dependent. Although some approaches reduce specific classes of weaknesses, they often introduce new weaknesses as side effects of the fixes. Moreover, no strategy consistently eliminates weaknesses across all models and scenarios, highlighting the absence of a universally effective "bulletproof" solution for secure AI-generated code.

</details>


### [103] [Evaluating Non-English Developer Support in Machine Learning for Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05902)
*Jonathan Katzy,Yongcheng Huang,Gopal-Raj Panchu,Maksym Ziemlewski,Paris Loizides,Sander Vermeulen,Arie van Deursen,Maliheh Izadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了大语言模型在非英语代码注释生成任务上的表现及评估方法的可靠性。研究发现：1）生成质量在非英语场景下显著下降，语言错误剧增；2）现有所有自动评估方法（重叠指标、神经指标、LLM-as-a-judge）均无法可靠评估非英语输出质量。这表明多语言代码工具的生成与评估都是关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前代码生成和评估工具仍以英语为中心，缺乏对多语言开发场景（代码中包含非英语自然语言）的理解。研究，旨在探索现有工具在非英语代码注释生成任务上的表现，以及现有评估方法对非英语输出的可靠性。

Method: 1. 对5种代码LLM（CodeGemma、CodeLlama、CodeQwen1.5、GraniteCode、StarCoder2）在5种自然语言（荷兰语、英语、希腊语、波兰语、汉语）上进行代码注释生成评估；2. 对12,500条生成注释进行开放式编码研究，构建包含26种错误类型的分类体系，并发布人工标注数据集；3. 使用人工标注评估多种自动评估方法的性能，包括基于重叠的指标、开箱即用的神经指标、扩展神经指标（多语言/语言特定/代码特定模型）以及LLM-as-a-judge流程。

Result: 1. 生成质量：非英语生成质量显著下降，语言错误增加高达15.1倍，常出现不连贯生成，语义错误增多；2. 评估可靠性：所有自动评估方法均无法可靠评估非英语注释；神经指标无法区分正确注释与错误输出甚至随机噪声，且在非英语场景下倾向于高估质量；LLM-as-a-judge与人工标注一致性最高，但无法可靠捕获重要的语言相关错误和语义错误。

Conclusion: 代码生成与评估工具在非英语场景下面临重大挑战。自动评估方法（基于重叠的指标、神经指标、LLM-as-a-judge）均无法可靠、一致地评估非英语代码注释质量。人类判断仍然是不可或缺的。

Abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly used in software engineering, but both code generation and its evaluation remain predominantly English-centric. This leaves a major gap in our understanding of how well current tools support multilingual development, where code contains non-English natural language. In this paper, we investigate non-English code comment generation and the reliability of current methods for evaluating such outputs. We evaluate five code LLMs (CodeGemma, CodeLlama, CodeQwen1.5, GraniteCode, and StarCoder2) across five natural languages: Dutch, English, Greek, Polish and Chinese. We further conduct an open-coding study of 12,500 generated comments, from which we derive a publicly released human-annotated dataset and a taxonomy of 26 error types. We use these human annotations, to evaluate the performance of neural metrics, and LLM-as-a-judge pipelines.
  Our findings show that generative performance deteriorates substantially outside English, with linguistic errors increasing by up to 15.1$\times$, alongside frequent incoherent generations and a rise in semantic errors. More critically, we show that detecting errors in non-English comments underperforms. Across classical overlap-based metrics, off-the-shelf neural metrics, extended neural metrics using newer multilingual, language-specific, and code-specific models, and LLM-as-a-judge pipelines, no automatic approach provides reliable and consistent assessment. Neural metrics fail to distinguish correct comments from incorrect outputs or even random noise, and tend to overestimate quality in non-English settings. LLM-as-a-judge methods achieve the highest agreement with human annotations but fail to reliably capture important language-related and semantic errors. Overall, our results show that evaluation and generation are key barriers for multilingual tooling, and that human judgment remains indispensable.

</details>


### [104] [Exploring the Effectiveness of Abstract Syntax Tree Patterns for Algorithm Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06098)
*Denis Neumüller,Florian Sihler,Raphael Straub,Matthias Tichy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于抽象语法树（AST）和领域特定语言（DSL）的自动化算法实现识别方法，通过DSL描述算法关键特征并在AST中匹配搜索。在BigCloneEval基准测试集上，该方法达到0.74的平均F1-score和0.62的召回率，显著优于大型语言模型CodeLlama（0.35 F1）和现有代码克隆检测工具（0.20召回率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法识别方法存在可用性和可扩展性问题，且缺乏准确性评估；同时，识别低效算法（如Bubble Sort）并建议更优替代方案有助于评估和改进系统质量。

Method: 1）设计了一种领域特定语言（DSL）用于描述算法关键特征和AST搜索模式；2）开发了模式匹配算法在AST中查找算法特征；3）构建了包含多种"即用型"模式的初始模式目录；4）在BigCloneEval基准测试集（包含Fibonacci、Bubble Sort、Binary Search等算法）上评估原型。

Result: 平均F1-score达到0.74，显著优于CodeLlama的0.35；召回率达到0.62，而最佳代码克隆检测工具仅为0.20。

Conclusion: 提出的基于AST和DSL的算法识别方法（F1=0.74）显著优于CodeLlama（LLaMA）（0.35）和最佳代码克隆检测工具（召回率0.20），展示了该方法在算法识别任务上的有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: The automated recognition of algorithm implementations can support many software maintenance and re-engineering activities by providing knowledge about the concerns present in the code base. Moreover, recognizing inefficient algorithms like Bubble Sort and suggesting superior alternatives from a library can help in assessing and improving the quality of a system. Approaches from related work suffer from usability as well as scalability issues and their accuracy is not evaluated. In this paper, we investigate how well our approach based on the abstract syntax tree of a program performs for automatic algorithm recognition. To this end, we have implemented a prototype consisting of: A domain-specific language designed to capture the key features of an algorithm and used to express a search pattern on the abstract syntax tree, a matching algorithm to find these features, and an initial catalog of "ready to use" patterns. To create our search patterns we performed a web search using the algorithm name and described key features of the found reference implementations with our domain-specific language. We evaluate our prototype on a subset of the BigCloneEval benchmark containing algorithms like Fibonacci, Bubble Sort, and Binary Search. We achieve an average F1-score of 0.74 outperforming the large language model Codellama which attains 0.35. Additionally, we use multiple code clone detection tools as a baseline for comparison, achieving a recall of 0.62 while the best-performing tool reaches 0.20.

</details>


### [105] [Schedule-and-Calibrate: Utility-Guided Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning for Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06111)
*Yujia Chen,Yang Ye,Xiao Chu,Yuchi Ma,Cuiyun Gao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ASTOR是一个基于实用驱动的多任务代码强化学习框架，通过层次化实用路由数据调度和自适应实用校准策略优化两个模块，解决了现有MTRL方法对所有编码任务统一处理、固定数据课程和共享优化策略的局限性，在两项LLM和四个编码任务上显著优于任务特定专家模型和多任务基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前为编码任务部署独立的专用模型成本高昂；现有MTRL方法对所有编码任务采用统一处理、固定数据课程和共享优化策略，无法充分发挥多任务训练效果。

Method: ASTOR框架以任务效用（衡量任务学习潜力和跨任务协同的信号）为核心，包含两个耦合模块：1）层次化实用路由数据调度模块，分层分配训练预算并优先处理信息量大的prompt；2）自适应实用校准策略优化模块，动态调整每任务的KL正则化强度以匹配当前训练状态。

Result: 在两个LLM和四个代表性编码任务上的实验表明：ASTOR在所有任务上持续提升单一模型性能，超越最佳任务特定专家模型9.0%-9.5%，并超过最强MTRL基线7.5%-12.8%。

Conclusion: ASTOR通过实用驱动的协调机制，有效解决了多任务代码RL中的任务不均衡和优化冲突问题，为统一多任务代码模型训练提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards has proven effective at post-training LLMs for coding, yet deploying separate task-specific specialists incurs costs that scale with the number of tasks, motivating a unified multi-task RL (MTRL) approach. However, existing MTRL methods treat all coding tasks uniformly, relying on fixed data curricula under a shared optimization strategy, ultimately limiting the effectiveness of multi-task training. To address these limitations, we propose ASTOR, a multi-tASk code reinforcement learning framework via uTility-driven coORdination. Centered on task utility, a signal capturing each task learning potential and cross-task synergy, ASTOR comprises two coupled modules: 1) Hierarchical Utility-Routed Data Scheduling module hierarchically allocates training budget and prioritizes informative prompts, steering training toward the most valuable data and 2) Adaptive Utility-Calibrated Policy Optimization module dynamically scales per-task KL regularization, matching update constraints to each tasks current training state. Experiments on two widely-used LLMs across four representative coding tasks demonstrate that ASTOR consistently improves a single model across all tasks, outperforming the best task-specific specialist by 9.0%-9.5% and surpassing the strongest MTRL baseline by 7.5%-12.8%.

</details>


### [106] [BUILD-AND-FIND: An Effort-Aware Protocol for Evaluating Agent-Managed Codebases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06136)
*Jhen-Ke Lin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: BUILD-AND-FIND是一个用于评估代码仓库可理解性的协议，通过构建者-检查者范式衡量下游智能体从生成代码库中恢复设计意图的能力，同时记录所需的审查工作量。协议通过准确率、稳定性和工作量等指标，区分代码的行为正确性与意图可恢复性，为代码生成质量提供更全面的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体基准测试仅评估生成代码的行为正确性，但忽略了仓库作为沟通工件的价值。即使下游智能体能够近乎完美地完成任务，如果代码库不能清晰暴露设计意图和选择，后续检查、审计或扩展工作将面临困难。因此需要区分"行为正确"与"意图可恢复"两个维度。

Method: 采用双角色范式：构建者根据隐藏的仓库规格创建代码库，检查者仅通过代码库和基于规格追踪的多选题库恢复意图。通过准确率和稳定性作为门槛条件，仅在恢复可靠成功时解释审查工作量差异，并设置仅问题组和仅规格组作为对照，辅以审计机制区分遗漏声明与检查者失误。

Result: 在高优先级任务包中，恢复准确率接近饱和，因此审查工作量差异和检查者特定效应成为主要的局部比较维度。这表明当前任务集已足够简单，下一步需要更具挑战性的任务来区分不同代码库的质量。

Conclusion: 该协议通过多维度指标（准确性、稳定性、覆盖率、审查工作量）区分行为正确性与工件侧恢复能力，为评估代码生成质量提供了更全面的框架，尤其适用于评估后续智能体能否有效理解和利用生成的代码库。

Abstract: Most coding-agent benchmarks ask whether generated code behaves correctly. That remains essential, but repository-level engineering is increasingly agent-managed: one agent writes a repository, and later agents inspect, audit, or extend it as working context. In that setting, a generated repository is not only an answer to a task but also a communication artifact for future work. Even when strong agents nearly satisfy the visible behavioral objective, repositories can differ in how clearly they expose the intended behavior and design choices behind that behavior. We introduce BUILD-AND-FIND, a protocol for evaluating whether downstream agents can recover those intended choices from generated repositories, and how much inspection that recovery requires. For each task, a builder sees a hidden repository specification and creates a codebase; a finder sees only the codebase and a specification-traced multiple-choice question bank. The protocol separates behavioral correctness from artifact-side recovery and reports recovery accuracy, repeatability, implementation coverage, and inspection effort. Accuracy and stability act as gates: effort is interpreted only when recovery succeeds reliably. Among artifacts from which the same intent can be recovered, lower effort by the same finder suggests that the artifact makes that intent easier to locate. Question-only and spec-only controls quantify generic priors and specification access, while audits separate omitted claims from finder failures and check whether correct answers cite artifact evidence. In the released high-prior task pack, recovery accuracy is near saturation, so inspection effort and finder-specific effects provide the main panel-local comparison.

</details>


### [107] [Teaching LLMs Program Semantics via Symbolic Execution Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06184)
*Jonas Bayer,Stefan Zetzsche,Olivier Bouissou,Remi Delmas,Michael Tautschnig,Soonho Kong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对大模型在C代码形式化验证中的薄弱环节——属性违反检测能力不足的问题，提出使用符号执行引擎生成的bug traces对Qwen3-8B进行继续预训练，并结合链式思维推理。约3000条bug traces即可使8B模型在违反检测任务上超越4倍规模的Qwen3-32B，且训练与推理策略的组合具有超加性效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型在代码属性验证任务中虽然整体准确率较高，但存在关键缺陷：属性违反（violation）检测能力差异大，且随程序长度增加性能急剧下降。这严重限制了模型在实际代码安全分析中的应用价值。

Method: 1. 构建500个C验证任务，涵盖5类属性（内存安全、溢出、终止性、可达性、数据竞争），基于SV-COMP 2025；2. 评估14个模型、6个模型家族；3. 使用Soteria符号执行引擎在开源C代码上生成bug traces，对Qwen3-8B进行继续预训练；4. 推理时采用链式思维（chain-of-thought）策略。

Result: 仅约3000条bug traces结合链式思维推理，使违反检测能力提升超过17个百分点，8B模型在违反检测上超越4倍规模的Qwen3-32B（不使用thinking模式），整体准确率也接近该大模型。训练与推理策略的组合效果呈超加性：单独使用任一方法提升有限，但组合使用效果显著。改进可迁移至所有5类属性，包括训练未直接针对的类型。

Conclusion: 1. 基于符号执行traces的继续预训练能有效提升模型的代码验证能力；2. 提升来源于trace语义而非代码数量，trace的筛选和格式设计至关重要；3. 链式思维推理与trace训练的组合是关键，二者缺一不可；4. 方法具有良好的泛化性，可迁移至多种属性类型。

Abstract: We introduce an evaluation framework of 500 C verification tasks across five property types (memory safety, overflow, termination, reachability, data races) built on SV-COMP 2025, and evaluate 14 models across six families. We find that high overall accuracy masks a critical weakness: while most models reliably confirm properties hold, violation detection varies widely and degrades sharply with program length. To close this gap, we train on formal verification artifacts: running the Soteria symbolic execution engine on generic open-source C code and using the resulting traces for continued pretraining of Qwen3-8B. Just ${\sim}$3,000 bug traces combined with chain-of-thought reasoning at inference time improve violation detection by over 17 percentage points, producing one of the most balanced accuracy profiles among evaluated models. On violation detection, the trained 8B model outperforms the 4$\times$ larger Qwen3-32B without thinking and approaches it in overall accuracy. The interaction between trace training and chain-of-thought is superadditive: neither alone provides meaningful gains, but their combination does. Improvements transfer across all five property types, including ones the training traces do not target. Our 28 configurations confirm the gains stem from trace semantics, not code volume, and that trace curation and format matter.

</details>


### [108] [SiblingRepair: Sibling-Based Multi-Hunk Repair with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06209)
*Xinyu Liu,Jiayu Ren,Yusen Wang,Qi Xin,Xiaoyuan Xie,Jifeng Xuan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SiblingRepair，一种新的LLM驱动的多hunk自动程序修复技术，专门用于兄弟修复。该技术通过token和embedding匹配搜索语义相关兄弟候选，使用LLM识别失败相关兄弟并生成一致补丁（同时/迭代策略），在Defects4J和GHRB上显著优于Hercules等SOTA技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA技术Hercules存在局限性：1)对兄弟位置和提交历史可用性的强假设；2)基于AST的刚性兄弟匹配；3)基于模板的僵硬补丁生成。这些限制阻碍了自动化程序修复的准确性和一致性。

Method: SiblingRepair采用LLM驱动的多hunk修复方法：1)基于token和embedding的代码匹配搜索语义相关的兄弟候选；2)通过LLM识别失败相关的兄弟；3)同时修复策略（联合修复兄弟）和迭代修复策略（渐进式分析候选构建补丁）；4)保留早期可疑位置的补丁并合并为泛化的多hunk补丁。

Result: SiblingRepair在Defects4J和GHRB基准测试中显著优于SOTA多hunk修复技术，证明了其修复效率、兄弟检测和修复组件的有效性，以及LLM数据泄漏的有限影响。

Conclusion: SiblingRepair在Defects4J和GHRB基准测试中显著优于包括Hercules在内的SOTA多hunk修复技术，展示了其修复效率、组件有效性和有限的LLM数据泄漏影响，为自动兄弟修复和通用多hunk修复领域做出了贡献。

Abstract: Developers often make similar mistakes across code locations implementing related functionalities. These locations, called siblings, share similar issues and require similar fixes. Accurately identifying siblings and consistently repairing them are crucial for automated program repair. Hercules is a SOTA technique designed for sibling repair. However, it is limited by strong assumptions about sibling locations and commit-history availability, rigid AST-based sibling matching, and inflexible template-based patch generation. To address these limitations, we present SiblingRepair, a new LLM-based multi-hunk APR technique specialized for sibling repair. Starting from a suspicious location identified by spectrum-based fault localization, SiblingRepair searches for semantically related sibling candidates using token- and embedding-based code matching, without restricting discovery to failing-test coverage or commit history. It then uses an LLM to identify failure-relevant siblings and generate consistent patches through two complementary strategies: simultaneous repair, which jointly repairs siblings, and iterative repair, which progressively analyzes candidates for patch construction. SiblingRepair further preserves promising patches generated from earlier suspicious locations and combines them into generalized multi-hunk patches. We evaluate SiblingRepair on the Defects4J and GHRB benchmarks. The results show that SiblingRepair substantially outperforms SOTA multi-hunk repair techniques including Hercules. Our evaluation further demonstrates its repair efficiency, the effectiveness of its sibling detection and repair components, and limited impact of the LLM data leakage on the results. Overall, SiblingRepair advances automated sibling and general multi-hunk repair.

</details>


### [109] [Correct Code, Vulnerable Dependencies: A Large Scale Measurement Study of LLM-Specified Library Versions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06279)
*Chengjie Wang,Jingzheng Wu,Xiang Ling,Tianyue Luo,Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究首次大规模分析了LLM生成Python代码中第三方库版本选择的安全与兼容风险。通过在1,000个Stack Overflow任务的基准测试集PinTrace上评估10个LLM，发现LLM指定的版本中存在大量已知CVE（36.70%-55.70%的任务），且多数具有高严重性，同时版本兼容率较低，揭示了LLM版本选择是一个被忽视但重要的风险面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM已被广泛应用于软件开发流程，但其生成的代码中包含的第三方库（TPL）版本选择可能带来安全和兼容风险，此前尚无系统研究。研究者希望首次对LLM版本级风险进行大规模测量研究。

Method: 构建了包含1,000个Stack Overflow编程任务的PinTrace基准测试集，评估了10个主流LLM。研究从版本指定率、漏洞暴露率、CVE公开时间、静态兼容性、动态测试通过率等多个维度进行分析，并测试了外部锚定版本约束的效果。

Result: （1）直接提示时版本指定率为26.83%-95.18%，创建依赖清单文件时为6.45%-59.19%；（2）指定版本中36.70%-55.70%的任务包含已知CVE，62.75%-74.51%为Critical或High严重性；（3）72.27%-91.37%的CVE在模型知识截止日期前已公开；（4）各模型收敛于相同的少量风险版本，表明存在系统性偏见；（5）静态兼容率19.70%-63.20%，动态测试通过率6.49%-48.62%；（6）外部锚定版本约束可显著降低漏洞暴露和兼容失败。

Conclusion: LLM的版本选择是一个此前被忽视的一级风险面，需要引起开发者和模型提供商的重视。所有模型都存在系统性偏见而非偶发错误。外部锚定的版本约束策略可有效缓解此类风险。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are now largely involved in software development workflows, and the code they generate routinely includes third-party library (TPL) imports annotated with specific version identifiers. These version choices can carry security and compatibility risks, yet they have not been systematically studied.
  We present the first large-scale measurement study of version-level risk in LLM-generated Python code, evaluating 10 LLMs on PinTrace, a curated benchmark of 1,000 Stack Overflow programming tasks. LLMs tend to specify version identifiers when directly prompted at 26.83%-95.18%, while down to 6.45%-59.19% in creating a manifest file directly. Among the specified versions, 36.70%-55.70% of tasks contain at least one known CVE, and 62.75%-74.51% of them carry Critical or High severity ratings. In 72.27%-91.37% of cases, the associated CVEs were publicly disclosed before the model's knowledge cutoff. The statistics show all models converge on the same small set of risky release versions, indicating a systemic bias rather than isolated model error. Static compatibility rates range from 19.70% to 63.20%, with installation failure as the dominant cause. The dynamic test cases confirm the pattern by 6.49%-48.62% pass rates. Further experiments confirm that these failures are attributable to version selection rather than code quality, and that externally anchored version constraints substantially reduce both vulnerability exposure and compatibility failures. Our findings reveal LLM version selection as a first-class, previously overlooked risk surface in LLM-based development. We disclosed these findings to the community of the evaluated models, and several confirmed the issue. All the code and dataset have been released for open science at https://github.com/dw763j/PinTrace.

</details>


### [110] [Constraint Decay: The Fragility of LLM Agents in Backend Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06445)
*Francesco Dente,Dario Satriani,Paolo Papotti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents demonstrate strong performance in autonomous code generation under loose specifications. However, production-grade software requires strict adherence to structural constraints, such as architectural patterns, databases, and object-relational mappings. Existing benchmarks often overlook these non-functional requirements, rewarding functionally correct but structurally arbitrary solutions. We present a systematic study evaluating how well agents handle structural constraints in multi-file backend generation. By fixing a unified API contract across 80 greenfield generation tasks and 20 feature-implementation tasks spanning eight web frameworks, we isolate the effect of structural complexity using a dual evaluation with end-to-end behavioral tests and static verifiers. Our findings reveal a phenomenon of constraint decay: as structural requirements accumulate, agent performance exhibits a substantial decline. Capable configurations lose 30 points on average in assertion pass rates from baseline to fully specified tasks, while some weaker configurations approach zero. Framework sensitivity analysis exposes significant performance disparities: agents succeed in minimal, explicit frameworks (e.g., Flask) but perform substantially worse on average in convention-heavy environments (e.g., FastAPI, Django). Finally, error analysis identifies data-layer defects (e.g., incorrect query composition and ORM runtime violations) as the leading root causes. This work highlights that jointly satisfying functional and structural requirements remains a key open challenge for coding agents.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [Towards Dependable Retrieval-Augmented Generation Using Factual Confidence Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05244)
*Florian Geissler,Francesco Carella,Laura Fieback,Jakob Spiegelberg*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出两阶段方法预测RAG输出的事实忠实度：第一阶段用共形预测筛选高置信度检索块，第二阶段用注意力分类器检测生成答案与检索上下文的一致性。该方法为构建认证型RAG系统提供了理论基础和实践方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统中检索到的上下文可能包含无关甚至误导性信息，导致生成答案出现事实性错误。需要为RAG系统的输出提供有意义的事实性置信度度量，以确保生成内容的可靠性。

Method: 采用两阶段方法：1) 使用共形预测(Conformal Prediction)筛选高置信度检索块；2) 使用基于注意力的真实性分类器(Attention-based Factuality Classifier)量化生成答案与检索上下文的语义一致性。

Result: 共形预测方法在部分数据集上可将答案质量提升6%；基于注意力的一致性检测器可识别高达77%的不一致答案。

Conclusion: 研究提出了两种技术方案来提升RAG系统的可靠性：共形预测用于过滤低质量检索结果，注意力机制分类器用于检测生成答案与检索上下文的矛盾。这为构建认证型RAG系统提供了方法论基础，但共形预测的交换性假设在实际应用中存在局限。

Abstract: Incorporating specific knowledge into large language models via retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is a widespread technique that fuels many of today's industry AI applications. A fundamental problem is to assess if the context retrieved by some similarity search provides indeed supporting facts, or instead misguides the generator with irrelevant information. It is critical to associate meaningful confidence measures about the factuality of the retrieval process with the generated answers. We present a new, two-staged approach to predict fact faithfulness of the output of retrieval-augmented generations. First, we employ conformal prediction to select only those retrieved chunks who have a high chance to come from the correct source. This approach in itself can improve answer quality by up to 6% in some of the studied datasets, however, the associated statistical guarantees do not hold generally, since the assumption of sample exchangeability depends on the retriever setup. We present diagnostic metrics to assess whether a setup is suitable. Second, we quantify confidence in the consistency of a generated final answer with a given retrieved context, using an attention-based factuality classifier. This approach can detect inconsistent answers with a chance of up to 77%. Our work helps to establish a novel type of certified RAG systems for a broad range of natural language industry applications.

</details>


### [112] [TriAlignGR: Triangular Multitask Alignment with Multimodal Deep Interest Mining for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05249)
*Yangchen Zeng,Hao Peng,Rongfeng Guo,Zhenyu Yu,Zhiyuan Hu,Jinze Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出TriAlignGR，一个统一的多任务多模态生成式推荐框架，通过两阶段多模态语义传播解决现有SID方法中的内容降级(SCD)和语义不透明(SSO)两大核心问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Semantic ID (SID)推荐管道存在两个未充分探索的根本性问题：(1) SID内容降级(SCD)：级联编码和残差量化丢弃了关键的多模态和兴趣级语义；(2) SID语义不透明(SSO)：模型自回归生成SID序列却未真正理解其底层含义，导致幻觉和泛化差。现有工作最多只解决文本-SID对齐，完全未利用视觉语义和潜在用户兴趣。

Method: TriAlignGR通过三个紧密集成的组件解决上述问题：(1) 跨模态语义对齐(CMSA)：通过VLM生成的文本描述和多模态嵌入模型将视觉内容整合到SID构建中；(2) 多模态深度兴趣挖掘(MDIM)：利用LLM思维链推理提取超越表面属性的潜在用户意图；(3) 三角多任务(TMT)：在单一自回归损失下联合训练8个互补生成任务，包括两个新颖的视觉语义任务(VisDesc→SID, VisDesc→Title)。

Result: SID内在携带多模态语义，完成了SID-Text-Image三角关系，无需任务特定塔或复杂损失加权。

Conclusion: TriAlignGR通过两阶段多模态语义传播机制，首次系统性地解决了SID推荐中的内容降级和语义不透明问题，实现视觉语义与文本语义在SID表示中的深度融合。

Abstract: We introduce TriAlignGR, a unified multitask-multimodal framework for generative recommendation that establishes two-stage multimodal semantic propagation: (i) encoding visual semantics directly into SIDs via multimodal embeddings, and (ii) enabling the model to decode these semantics through visual description tasks. Existing Semantic ID (SID) pipelines suffer from two fundamental but underexplored problems: \textbf{SID Content Degradation (SCD)}, where cascaded encoding and residual quantization discard critical multimodal and interest-level semantics; and \textbf{SID Semantic Opacity (SSO)}, where models autoregressively generate SID sequences without truly comprehending their underlying meaning, leading to hallucination and poor generalization. Prior work addresses at most text-SID alignment, leaving visual semantics and latent user interests entirely unexploited. TriAlignGR resolves both problems through three tightly integrated components: (1)~\textbf{Cross-Modal Semantic Alignment (CMSA)} integrates visual content into SID construction through both VLM-generated textual descriptions and a multimodal embedding model that directly encodes image features alongside text, ensuring that SIDs inherently carry multimodal semantics; (2)~\textbf{Multimodal Deep Interest Mining (MDIM)} leverages LLM Chain-of-Thought reasoning to extract latent user intents (\eg ``productivity-focused lifestyle'' from noise-canceling headphones) beyond surface attributes, enriching SID semantics before discretization; and (3)~\textbf{Triangular Multitask (TMT)} jointly trains on eight complementary generation tasks under a single autoregressive loss -- including two novel visual-semantic tasks (VisDesc$\to$SID, VisDesc$\to$Title) that map VLM-generated image descriptions to SIDs and titles, completing the SID-Text-Image triangle -- without requiring task-specific towers or complex loss weighting.

</details>


### [113] [Decision-aware User Simulation Agent for Evaluating Conversational Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05250)
*Yuan-Chi Li,Li-Chi Chen,Sung-Yi Wu,Yu-Che Tsai,Shou-De Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Hesitator框架，通过显式建模人类决策过程中的犹豫行为来解决现有对话推荐系统用户模拟器过于"完美"的问题。该框架引入模块化决策模块，在多个实验设置下验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户模拟器缺乏对内部决策过程的显式建模，LLM基础模拟器表现出不切实际的强信息处理能力，罕见表现出犹豫或决策延迟，导致接受概率过高。

Method: 引入模块化决策模块，将基于效用的项目选择与过载感知承诺决策分离；在多个模拟框架、领域、销售模式和LLM后端上进行实验验证。

Result: 集成该模块持续缓解了递增过载条件下的不现实行为，并成功复现心理学经济学中的经典行为模式。

Conclusion: Hesitator框架通过模块化设计有效缓解了模拟用户在不实信息处理能力和接受概率过高的问题，实验证明其能够复现心理学经济学中的经典行为模式。

Abstract: Conversational recommender systems (CRS) increasingly rely on user simulators for automated evaluation of sales agents. A key requirement for such simulators is the ability to model human decision-making. However, most existing simulation frameworks do not explicitly model the internal decision process, and LLM-based simulators often exhibit unrealistically strong information-processing capabilities, rarely exhibit the hesitation or decision deferral commonly observed in real consumer behavior, resulting in overly high acceptance probabilities. To address this limitation, we propose Hesitator, a theory-grounded user simulation framework that explicitly models human decision-making under choice overload. The framework introduces a modular Decision Module that separates utility-based item selection from overload-aware commitment decisions. Experiments across multiple user simulation frameworks, domains, sales modes, and LLM backbones show that integrating our module consistently mitigates unrealistic behaviors under increasing overload conditions. Furthermore, Hesitator reproduces established behavioral patterns from psychological economics, demonstrating its ability to model human decision behavior.

</details>


### [114] [EnterpriseRAG-Bench: A RAG Benchmark for Company Internal Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05253)
*Yuhong Sun,Joachim Rahmfeld,Chris Weaver,Roshan Desai,Wenxi Huang,Mark H. Butler*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了EnterpriseRAG-Bench，一个用于评估企业场景下检索增强生成（RAG）系统的基准数据集，包含约50万文档和500个问题，涵盖9种企业数据源类型，以弥补现有RAG基准数据集缺乏真实企业环境模拟的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RAG评估数据集主要基于网页或公开数据源，缺乏对企业内部专有数据场景的真实反映。而初创公司、企业和研究人员正越来越多地开发针对此类专有数据的企业AI Agent，现有基准无法满足这一需求。

Method: 构建包含约50万文档的综合企业语料库，覆盖9种企业数据源类型（Slack、Gmail、Linear、Google Drive、HubSpot、Fireflies、GitHub、Jira、Confluence）；设计500个问题，涵盖10个类别，测试不同的检索和推理能力；引入跨文档一致性（基于共享项目、人员、计划）和真实噪声（错放文档、近重复、冲突信息）；提供可定制化的生成框架。

Result: 提出了一个全面的企业RAG基准数据集，包含多样化数据源、多种问题类型、真实噪声场景和可扩展的生成框架，支持多文档推理、约束检索、冲突解决和信息缺失识别等能力评估。

Conclusion: EnterpriseRAG-Bench为评估企业环境中的RAG系统提供了真实基准，支持团队根据自身行业、规模和资源类型定制数据集，代码、评估工具和排行榜已开源发布。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become the standard approach for grounding large language models in information that was not available during training. While existing datasets and benchmarks focus on web or other public sources, there is still no widely adopted dataset that realistically reflects the nature of company-internal knowledge. Meanwhile, startups, enterprises, and researchers are increasingly developing AI Agents designed to operate over exactly this kind of proprietary data. To close this gap, we release a synthetic enterprise corpus, its generation framework, and a leaderboard.
  We present EnterpriseRAG-Bench, a dataset consisting of approximately 500,000 documents spanning nine enterprise source types (Slack, Gmail, Linear, Google Drive, HubSpot, Fireflies, GitHub, Jira, and Confluence) and 500 questions across ten categories that test distinct retrieval and reasoning capabilities. The corpus is generated with cross-document coherence (grounded in shared projects, people, and initiatives) and augmented with realistic noise such as misfiled documents, near-duplicates, and conflicting information. The question set ranges from simple single-document lookups to multi-document reasoning, constrained retrieval, conflict resolution, and recognizing when information is absent. The generation framework lets teams generate variants tailored to their own industry, scale, and source mix. The dataset, code, evaluation harness, and leaderboard are available at https://github.com/onyx-dot-app/EnterpriseRAG-Bench.

</details>


### [115] [Effective Knowledge Transfer for Multi-Task Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05730)
*Guohao Cai,Jun Yuan,Zhenhua Dong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出EKTM多任务推荐知识迁移方法，通过路由-发射双模块架构实现跨任务知识共享，解决CVR任务数据稀疏问题。离线指标和在线A/B测试均验证有效，eCPM提升3.93%，已工业部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CVR是衡量平台内容与用户偏好匹配度的重要指标，但用户转化行为数据的稀疏性给排序模型训练带来显著挑战。

Method: 提出EKTM方法，包含三个核心组件：1）路由模块，用于整合和分发跨任务知识；2）发射模块，每个CVR任务配备此模块以实现从路由模块的知识转换；3）增强模块，确保迁移知识能反哺原任务学习。

Result: 离线实验中超越现有最优方法；在线A/B测试在商业平台实现eCPM提升3.93%，已全量部署于平台两个主要流量场景。

Conclusion: EKTM方法已在商业平台的工业场景中验证有效并全面部署，证明了跨任务知识迁移对多任务推荐系统的实际价值。

Abstract: The conversion rate (CVR) is a crucial metric for evaluating the effectiveness of platforms, as it quantifies the alignment of content with audience preferences. However, the limited nature of customers' conversion actions presents a significant challenge for training ranking models effectively. In this paper, we propose an Effective Knowledge Transfer method for Multi-task Recommendation Models (EKTM). This method enables the ranking model to learn from diverse user behaviors, thereby enhancing performance through the transfer of knowledge across distinct yet related tasks. Each specific CVR task can directly benefit from the insights provided by other tasks. To achieve this, we first introduce a router module that integrates and disseminates knowledge across tasks. Subsequently, each CVR task is equipped with a transmitter module that facilitates the transformation of knowledge from the router. Additionally, we propose an enhanced module to ensure that the transferred knowledge benefit the original task learning. Extensive experiments on several benchmark datasets demonstrate that our proposed method outperforms existing state-of-the-art approaches. Online A/B testing on a commercial platform has validated the effectiveness of the EKTM algorithm in large-scale industrial settings, resulting in a 3.93% uplift in effective Cost Per Mille (eCPM). The algorithm has since been fully deployed across two of the platform's main-traffic scenarios.

</details>


### [116] [Beyond Long Tail POIs: Transition-Centered Generalization for Human Mobility Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05771)
*Dingyang Lyu,Zhengjia Xu,Jey Han Lau,Jianzhong Qi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文研究人类移动性预测中的长尾问题，核心发现是预测失败不仅来自长尾POI，还来自长尾转移（source-destination transition），并提出RECAP框架通过重建长尾转移来改善预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注长尾POI（访问记录少的POI），但作者发现即使对热门POI的访问也经常预测失败，根本原因是转移级别稀疏（transition-level sparsity）：源-目的转移对在训练集中很少或从未出现。

Method: 提出RECAP框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 从两个可泛化信号重建长尾转移：全局转移图中的多跳传递性和用户历史轨迹中的重访证据；(2) 使用warm-transition保留训练策略，抑制对频繁转移的记忆，促进从可迁移信号泛化。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上的实验表明，RECAP一致提升预测准确性，特别是在长尾转移上有明显改善。

Conclusion: 将人类移动性预测中的长尾问题重新定义为组合泛化问题，通过转移重建和泛化学习能有效解决转移级别稀疏的瓶颈。

Abstract: Human mobility prediction forecasts a user's next Point of Interest (POI) from historical trajectories, supporting applications from recommendation to urban planning. Recent studies have recognized the problem with long-tail POIs in human mobility prediction, which are POIs with few visit records, making new visits to such POIs difficult to predict. Our analysis shows that many predictions fail even for visits to popular POIs. The underlying cause is often transition-level sparsity: the corresponding source-destination transition appears rarely, or never appears, in the training set. We therefore argue that a core bottleneck in human mobility prediction lies in transition-level long-tail generalization. We formulate this problem as compositional generalization and propose a tRansition rEconstruction framework for Compositional generAlization in next-POI prediction (RECAP). RECAP reconstructs long-tail transitions from two generalizable signals: multi-hop transitivity in the global transition graph and revisit evidence from a user's historical trajectory. It further uses warm-transition holdout training to discourage memorization of frequent transitions and encourage generalization from transferable signals. Experiments on multiple real-world datasets show that RECAP consistently improves prediction accuracy, with clear gains on tail transitions.

</details>


### [117] [Unified Value Alignment for Generative Recommendation in Industrial Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05803)
*Xinxun Zhang,Yuling Xiong,Jiale Zhou,Zhengkai Guo,Zhennan Pang,Junbang Huo,Jingwen Wang,Xuyang Sun,Enming Zhang,Jiaguang Jin,Changping Wang,Yi Li,Jun Zhang,Xiao Yan,Jiawei Jiang,Jie Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: UniVA是首个将价值对齐统一融入生成式广告推荐全流程的框架，通过Commercial SID tokenizer、Generation-as-Ranking decoder和value-guided beam search三阶段创新，在微信频道广告平台实现显著业务提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐(GR)扩展到工业广告面临挑战——需同时优化用户兴趣与商业价值，现有GR流程以语义为核心，难以在tokenization、decoding和在线服务各阶段对齐价值信号。

Method: 提出UniVA框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) Commercial SID tokenizer：在SID构建中注入价值相关属性；(2) Generation-as-Ranking SID Decoder：结合监督学习与eCPM感知强化学习，将价值分数融合到SID生成中；(3) Value-guided personalized beam search：复用生成排序logits作为在线价值引导，使用个性化trie树约束解码路径。

Result: 离线Hit Rate@100提升37.04%，在线A/B测试GMV提升1.5%。

Conclusion: UniVA成功地将价值信号对齐贯穿整个生成式推荐流程，在微信频道广告平台实现了显著的业务提升。

Abstract: Generative Recommendation (GR) reformulates recommendation as a next-token generation problem and has shown promise in industrial applications. However, extending GR to industrial advertising is non-trivial because the system must optimize not only user interest but also commercial value. Existing GR pipelines remain largely semantics-centric, making it difficult to align value signals across tokenization, decoding, and online serving. To address this issue, we propose UniVA, a Unified Value Alignment framework for advertising recommendation. We first introduce a Commercial SID tokenizer that injects value-related attributes into SID construction, yielding value-discriminative item representations. We then develop a Generation-as-Ranking SID Decoder jointly optimized by supervised learning and eCPM-aware reinforcement learning, which fuses value scores into next-item SID generation to perform generation and ranking in one decoding process. Finally, we design a value-guided personalized beam search that reuses generation-as-ranking logits as online value guidance and applies a personalized trie tree to constrain decoding to request-valid SID paths. Experiments on the Tencent WeChat Channels advertising platform show that UniVA achieves a 37.04\% improvement in offline Hit Rate@100 over the baseline and a 1.5\% GMV lift in online A/B tests.

</details>


### [118] [Bridging Passive and Active: Enhancing Conversation Starter Recommendation via Active Expression Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05855)
*Yiqing Wu,Haoming Li,Guanyu Jiang,Jiahao Liang,Yongchun Zhu,Jingwu Chen,Feng Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型驱动的对话搜索中会话启动器推荐系统存在的"曝光-点击"反馈回路陷阱问题，提出PA-Bridge框架，通过对抗分布对齐器和语义离散化器，将用户主动查询中的开放世界意图纳入推荐系统，以打破信息茧房效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统会话启动器推荐依赖封闭的"曝光-点击"反馈回路，导致系统陷入信息茧房，在数据稀疏条件下无法捕捉开放世界对话搜索意图的动态变化，倾向于推荐热门但泛化的建议。

Method: (1) 对抗分布对齐器：桥接被动推荐启动器与主动用户表达之间的分布差异；(2) 语义离散化器：将开放文本语义映射到可计算的离散空间，使基于流行度的去偏算法能够在工业级流式训练中部署。

Result: 在线A/B测试表明，PA-Bridge显著提升了功能渗透率(Feature Penetration Rate) 0.54%，并增加了用户活跃天数。

Conclusion: 通过利用用户"自由意志"（主动查询）中的开放世界意图，可有效打破传统反馈回路陷阱，为对话搜索推荐系统提供新的范式转变。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-driven conversational search is shifting information retrieval from reactive keyword matching to proactive, open-ended dialogues. In this context, Conversation Starters are widely deployed to provide personalized query recommendations that help users initiate dialogues. Conventionally, recommending these starters relies on a closed "exposure-click" loop. Yet, this feedback loop mechanism traps the system in an echo chamber where, compounded by data sparsity, it fails to capture the dynamic nature of conversational search intents shaped by the open world. As a result, the system skews towards popular but generic suggestions.In this work, we uncover an untapped paradigm shift to shatter this harmful feedback loop: harnessing user "free will" through active user expressions. Unlike traditional recommendations, conversational search empowers users to bypass menus entirely through manually typed queries. The open-world intents in active queries hold the key to breaking this loop. However, incorporating them is non-trivial: (1) there exists an inherent distribution shift between active queries and formulated starters. (2) Furthermore, the "non-ID-able" nature of open text renders traditional item-based popularity statistics ineffective for large-scale industrial streaming training. To this end, we propose Passive-Active Bridge (PA-Bridge), a novel framework that employs an adversarial distribution aligner to bridge the distributional gap between passively recommended starters and active expressions. Moreover, we introduce a semantic discretizer to enable the deployment of popularity debiasing algorithms. Online A/B tests on our platform, demonstrate that PA-Bridge significantly boosts the Feature Penetration Rate by 0.54% and User Active Days

</details>


### [119] [A Case-Driven Multi-Agent Framework for E-Commerce Search Relevance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05991)
*Global E-Commerce Search Relevance Team*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出用多智能体系统替代传统人工闭环，实现电商搜索相关性从坏case识别到解决的端到端自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电商搜索相关性优化依赖产品经理、标注员、算法工程师、评估者等多角色协作的闭环流程，效率低且难以规模化。论文提出核心问题：能否用自主智能体替代人类角色实现pipeline自动化。

Method: 提出案例驱动的多智能体框架，包含User Agent（对话识别坏case）、Annotator Agent（多轮标注）、Optimizer Agent（自动分析与解决）；配合Harness-Engineering范式，包括统一检索-排序模型、指令遵循模型、Global Memory、Deep Search Agent及人-智能体协作对话系统。

Result: 人工评估表明框架能有效完成相关性任务，提升标注准确率，实现更及时、可泛化的坏case解决，证明了工业级应用的可行性。

Conclusion: 多智能体框架代表了LLM+Agent技术在电商搜索相关性优化场景中的系统性落地，为工业搜索 Relevance 优化提供了新的实践范式。

Abstract: Relevance is a foundation of user experience in e-commerce search. We view relevance optimization as a closed-loop ecosystem involving multiple human roles: users who provide feedback, product managers who define standards, annotators who label data, algorithm engineers who optimize models, and evaluators who assess performance. Because improving relevance in practice means systematically resolving user-perceived bad cases, we ask a system-level question: can this ecosystem be reimagined by replacing its human roles with autonomous agents? To answer this question, we propose a case-driven multi-agent framework that automates the pipeline from bad-case identification to resolution. The framework instantiates an Annotator Agent for multi-turn annotation, an Optimizer Agent for autonomous bad-case analysis and resolution, and a User Agent that identifies bad cases through conversational interaction, together forming an autonomous and continually evolving system. To make the framework practical in production, we further adopt a harness-engineering paradigm and build a unified retrieval-and-ranking relevance model for efficient training, an instruction-following relevance model for real-time case resolution, Global Memory to reduce information asymmetry across agents, a Deep Search Agent to target underestimation failures, and an agent-based chatbot for human--agent collaboration. Extensive human evaluation shows that the framework performs relevance-related tasks effectively, improves annotation accuracy, and enables more timely and generalizable bad-case resolution, indicating a practical paradigm for industrial search relevance optimization.

</details>


### [120] [OBLIQ-Bench: Exposing Overlooked Bottlenecks in Modern Retrievers with Latent and Implicit Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06235)
*Diane Tchuindjo,Devavrat Shah,Omar Khattab*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出倾斜查询（oblique queries）这一新型查询类型，其特点是寻找符合潜在抽象模式的文档，而非基于显式关键词匹配。通过构建OBLIQ-Bench基准（含5个真实长尾语料上的任务）进行系统评估，发现当前检索系统存在根本性局限：能识别但难以召回，这为隐式模式检索研究指明了新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索基准已趋于饱和，但高效搜索远未解决。研究发现一类特殊查询——倾斜查询（oblique queries），其目标是找出符合潜在模式的文档（如隐式立场的推文、特定失败模式的聊天记录、匹配抽象场景的转录文本等），这类查询对现有检索系统构成重大挑战。

Method: 研究识别了三类导致查询倾斜（obliqueness）的机制，并构建了OBLIQ-Bench基准，包含五个基于真实长尾语料库的倾斜搜索任务，用以系统性评估检索系统在隐式模式捕捉上的能力。

Result: 揭示了检索与验证之间被忽视的不对称性：推理型LLM在获得相关文档后能可靠地识别隐式相关性，但即便采用复杂的检索管道，在第一阶段也难以召回大部分相关文档。

Conclusion: OBLIQ-Bench揭示了当前检索系统与验证系统之间的关键不对称性，即推理型LLM在验证阶段能可靠识别隐含相关性，但检索阶段本身就无法有效召回相关文档。该基准套件有望推动针对隐式模式和模糊信号的新型检索架构研究。

Abstract: Retrieval benchmarks are increasingly saturating, but we argue that efficient search is far from a solved problem. We identify a class of queries we call oblique, which seek documents that instantiate a latent pattern, like finding all tweets that express an implicit stance, chat logs that demonstrate a particular failure mode, or transcripts that match an abstract scenario. We study three mechanisms through which obliqueness may arise and introduce OBLIQ-Bench, a suite of five oblique search problems over real long-tail corpora. OBLIQ-Bench exposes an overlooked asymmetry between retrieval and verification, where reasoning LLMs reliably recognize latent relevance whenever relevant documents are surfaced, but even sophisticated retrieval pipelines fail to surface most relevant documents in the first place. We hope that OBLIQ-Bench will drive research into retrieval architectures that efficiently capture latent patterns and implicit signals in large corpora.

</details>


### [121] [Expressiveness Limits of Autoregressive Semantic ID Generation in Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06331)
*Yupeng Hou,Haven Kim,Clark Mingxuan Ju,Eduardo Escoto,Neil Shah,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了生成式推荐模型中解码树结构导致的概率耦合问题，提出Latte方法通过注入潜在标记重构解码空间，在NDCG@10指标上提升约3.45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐模型的逐token自回归生成会形成解码树结构，导致树上距离近的item获得相似概率，难以基于用户偏好进行有效区分，且这种结构相关性使模型无法表示协同过滤可捕获的简单模式。

Method: Latte在语义ID标记前注入潜在标记，将解码空间从单一树结构重构为多个以潜在标记为条件的条件树，为每个item创建具有不同树距离的多条路径，从而放松树诱导的概率耦合约束。

Result: 相比基线模型，Latte在NDCG@10上实现平均3.45%的相对提升。

Conclusion: Latte通过在语义ID前注入潜在标记，将单一解码树重构为多个条件树，有效缓解了树结构带来的概率耦合问题，在NDCG@10上实现3.45%的相对提升。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) models generate items by autoregressively producing a sequence of discrete tokens that jointly index the target item. However, this autoregressive generation process also induces a structured decoding space whose impact on model expressiveness remains underexplored. Specifically, token-by-token generation can be viewed as traversing a decoding tree induced by semantic ID tokens, where leaf nodes correspond to candidate items. We observe that the item probabilities produced by GR models are strongly correlated with this tree structure: items that are close in the tree tend to receive similar probabilities for any given user, making it difficult to distinguish among them based on user-specific preferences. We further show theoretically that such structural correlations prevent GR models from representing even simple patterns that can be well captured by conventional collaborative filtering models. To mitigate this issue, we propose Latte, a simple modification that injects a latent token before each semantic ID, reshaping the decoding space from a single tree into multiple latent-token-conditioned trees. This design creates multiple paths with varying tree distances between items, relaxing tree-induced probability coupling and yielding an average of 3.45% relative improvement on NDCG@10. Our code is available at https://github.com/hyp1231/Latte.

</details>


### [122] [Light-FMP: Lightweight Feature and Model Pruning for Enhanced Deep Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06441)
*Nghia Bui,Yue Ning,Lijing Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Light-FMP是一种用于深度推荐系统的轻量级框架，通过预训练、剪枝和继续训练三个阶段，结合硬连续分布和掩码层来平衡计算效率和模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度推荐系统(DRS)在处理高维输入特征时面临计算效率与模型精度难以平衡的挑战。现有方法要么牺牲效率追求精度，要么牺牲精度换取效率，难以同时兼顾两者。

Method: 采用硬连续(Hard Concrete)分布和掩码层，在小规模数据子集上进行高效预训练以识别重要特征；对模型和特征进行剪枝后，使用领域适应参数在剩余数据集上继续训练。

Result: 在真实推荐系统基准数据集上的实验表明，Light-FMP在效率和精度方面均优于现有方法，同时保持了良好的可扩展性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Light-FMP框架通过特征与模型联合剪枝策略，有效解决了深度推荐系统中效率-精度的权衡问题，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Deep recommender systems (DRS) often face challenges in balancing computational efficiency and model accuracy, especially when handling high-dimensional input features. Existing methods either focus on improving accuracy while neglecting training efficiency or prioritize efficiency at the cost of suboptimal accuracy across tasks. We propose Light-FMP: Lightweight Feature and Model Pruning for Enhanced DRS, a lightweight framework that addresses the challenges through three key phases: \textit{pretraining}, \textit{pruning}, and \textit{continued training}. Using a hard concrete distribution, a masking layer is efficiently pretrained on a small data subset to identify important features. The model and features are then pruned, and training continues on the remaining dataset with domain-adapted parameters. Experiments on benchmark datasets from real-world recommender systems demonstrate that Light-FMP outperforms existing methods in both efficiency and accuracy while maintaining scalability and robustness.

</details>


### [123] [Superintelligent Retrieval Agent: The Next Frontier of Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06647)
*Zeyu Yang,Qi Ma,Jason Chen,Anshumali Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SIRA通过LLM认知和轻量级语料统计的引导，将多轮探索性检索压缩为单一精心设计的词汇查询，在BEIR基准和QA任务上显著超越密集检索器和多轮智能体方案，同时保持可解释性、无需训练且高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前检索增强智能体将检索视为黑盒，采用类似新手搜索数据库的探索性查询方式，需要迭代重写查询直到发现有用证据。这种方式模拟的是新手行为而非专家行为——专家拥有关于术语和可能证据的强先验知识。结果导致不必要的检索轮次、延迟增加和召回率下降。

Method: SIRA采用"超级智能"检索策略，将多轮探索性搜索压缩为单一语料库判别性检索动作。具体方法包括：（1）语料库端：LLM离线为每个文档补充缺失的搜索词汇；（2）查询端：LLM预测查询遗漏的证据词汇；（3）使用文档频率统计作为工具，过滤掉缺失、过于常见或无法产生检索增益的候选术语；（4）最终执行单一加权BM25调用，结合原始查询与验证后的扩展词汇。

Result: 在十个BEIR基准测试和下游问答任务中，SIRA显著优于密集检索器和最先进的的多轮智能体基线方法。

Conclusion: SIRA通过精心设计的术语选择机制（而非迭代搜索）实现了高效的检索，在保持可解释性、无需训练和高效率的同时，显著超越了密集检索器和多轮智能体基线方法。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented agents are increasingly the interface to large organizational knowledge bases, yet most still treat retrieval as a black box: they issue exploratory queries, inspect returned snippets, and iteratively reformulate until useful evidence emerges. This approach resembles how a newcomer searches an unfamiliar database rather than how an expert navigates it with strong priors about terminology and likely evidence, and results in unnecessary retrieval rounds, increased latency, and poor recall.
  We introduce \textit{SuperIntelligent Retrieval Agent} (SIRA), which defines \emph{superintelligence} in retrieval as the ability to compress multi-round exploratory search into a single corpus-discriminative retrieval action. SIRA does not merely ask what terms are relevant to the query; it asks which terms are likely to separate the desired evidence from corpus-level confusers. On the corpus side, an LLM enriches each document offline with missing search vocabulary; on the query side, it predicts evidence vocabulary omitted by the query; and document-frequency statistics as a tool call to filter proposed terms that are absent, overly common, or unlikely to create retrieval margin. The final retrieval step is a single weighted BM25 call combining the original query with the validated expansion.
  Across ten BEIR benchmarks and downstream question-answering tasks, SIRA achieves the significantly superior performance outperforming dense retrievers and state-of-the-art multi-round agentic baselines, demonstrating that one well-formed lexical query, guided by LLM cognition and lightweight corpus statistics, can exceed substantially more expensive multi-round search while remaining interpretable, training-free, and efficient.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [FinRAG-12B: A Production-Validated Recipe for Grounded Question Answering in Banking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05482)
*Denys Katerenchuk,Pablo Duboue,Keelan Evanini,David Gondek,Nithin Govindugari,Olivier Allauzen,Joshua Baptiste,David J More,Joshua Schechter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的、数据高效的框架，用于训练面向银行业务的领域特定LLM，通过143M token的标注数据结合LLM-as-Judge、引用标注和课程学习，在12B模型上实现超越GPT-4.1的答案质量和引用准确性，并通过22%的不可回答样本训练实现安全的拒绝机制，最终在40+金融机构部署，查询解决率提升7.1个百分点，推理速度提升3-5倍，成本降低20-50倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在银行业的应用面临阻力，主要挑战包括：高准确性要求、监管合规需求、以及对可验证、有据可查的响应需求。这些行业特定需求使得通用LLM难以直接应用。

Method: 1）数据生成流水线：结合LLM-as-Judge过滤、引用标注和课程学习，仅用143M token；2）校准拒绝机制：使用22%不可回答样本进行训练；3）端到端方法论：涵盖从数据策展到量化服务的全流程。

Result: 12B模型在引用准确性上超越GPT-4.1；拒绝率从基线的4.3%改善至12%，避免GPT-4.1的过度拒绝（20.2%）；在40+金融机构部署，查询解决率提升7.1个百分点（p<0.001）；响应速度提升3-5倍，成本降低20-50倍。

Conclusion: 该框架成功平衡了答案质量、引用可靠性和安全的拒绝能力，在真实银行业部署中验证了有效性，为领域特定LLM的合规部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly being adopted across various domains. However, their adoption in banking industry faces resistance due to demands for high accuracy, regulatory compliance, and the need for verifiable and grounded responses. We present a unified, data-efficient framework for training grounded domain-specific LLMs that optimizes answer quality, citation grounding, and calibrated refusal under real-world deployment constraints. First, we describe a data generation pipeline that combines LLM-as-a-Judge filtering, citation annotation, and curriculum learning with only 143M tokens. The resulting 12B model achieves high answer quality outperforming GPT-4.1 on citation grounding, with a modest citation tradeoff versus the untuned base. Second, we propose a calibrated refusal mechanism: training on 22% unanswerable examples yield a 12% "I don't know" rate, substantially improving over the base model's unsafe 4.3% rate while avoiding GPT-4.1's over-refusal (20.2%). Third, we present an end-to-end methodology spanning from data curation to quantized serving. The system is deployed at 40+ financial institutions, achieving a 7.1 percentage point improvement in query resolution (p < 0.001). Additionally, the model delivers 3-5x faster responses at 20-50x lower cost compared to GPT-4.1.

</details>


### [125] [Retrieval-Conditioned Topology Selection with Provable Budget Conservation for Multi-Agent Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05657)
*Abhijit Talluri,Pujith Anne,Bhagavan Choudary Pendiyala,Raghavendra Chilukuri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems for code generation face a fundamental routing problem: the optimal orchestration topology depends on the structural complexity of the code under modification, yet existing systems select topologies without consulting the codebase. We present Retrieval-Guided Adaptive Orchestration (RGAO), an architecture that closes this loop by extracting a structural complexity vector from a hierarchical code index before selecting the orchestration topology. RGAO operates within Code-Agent, a multi-agent framework whose sub-agents are governed by formal contracts with six-dimensional budget vectors. Our headline contribution is the composition of two previously separate lines of work -- complexity-conditioned LLM routing and formal resource algebras -- yielding a property neither admits alone: provable budget conservation under retrieval-conditioned dynamic topology selection. Concretely we contribute: (1) a complexity-conditioned topology router that reduces proxy-measured misrouting from 30.1% to 8.2%; (2) a budget algebra with a structural-induction conservation theorem; and (3) a hierarchical code retrieval engine. Empirical evaluation demonstrates sub-millisecond DAG construction and linear tree-index scalability.

</details>


### [126] [BioResearcher: Scenario-Guided Multi-Agent for Translational Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05985)
*Remigiusz Kinas,Joanna Krawczyk,Rafał Powalski,Przemysław Pietrzak,Agnieszka Kowalewska,Krzysztof Kolmus,Maciej Sypetkowski,Łukasz Smoliński,Tomasz Jetka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Ingenix BioResearcher is a scenario-guided multi-agent system designed for translational medicine, combining 30+ specialized tools with versioned research playbooks and multi-model reconciliation to perform auditable biomedical research workflows. It demonstrates strong performance across unit-level tests, biomedical benchmarks, and clinical discovery tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current general-purpose foundation models and off-the-shelf tool-augmented or multi-agent systems are inadequate for translational medicine. These systems tend to produce single-shot answers or run open-endedly, lacking the auditable, scenario-specific workflows required to handle heterogeneous biomedical sources (literature, trials, patents, quantitative multi-omics) while preserving identifiers, uncertainty, and retrievable provenance.

Method: The authors propose a scenario-guided multi-agent architecture called BioResearcher, which maps queries to versioned research playbooks, delegates tasks to 30+ specialized subagents and ML endpoints, combines structured database access with sandboxed code execution for genome-scale analyses, and applies claim-level multi-model reconciliation before final editorial assembly.

Result: BioResearcher achieved: (1) 83.49% pass rate with 0.892 average score on 109 single-step tests; (2) 89.33% on BixBench-Verified-50 and top 0.758 mean score on BioBench Scientific Discovery; (3) 74.7% ± 3.3% positive hit rate and 96.8% ± 0.2% negative clear rate on a 30-query clinical end-to-end benchmark.

Conclusion: Ingenix BioResearcher demonstrates broad and competitive performance across unit-level, open-ended, and end-to-end clinical evaluations, effectively addressing the gap between general-purpose AI systems and the specialized demands of translational biomedical research workflows.

Abstract: Translational medicine turns underspecified development goals into evidence synthesis that must combine literature, trials, patents, and quantitative multi-omics analysis while preserving identifiers, uncertainty, and retrievable provenance. General-purpose foundation models and off-the-shelf tool-augmented or multi-agent systems are not built for this: they tend to produce single-shot answers or run open-endedly, and fall short on the auditable, scenario-specific workflows that heterogeneous biomedical sources demand.
  This paper introduces Ingenix BioResearcher, a scenario-guided multi-agent system that maps queries to versioned research playbooks, delegates to specialized subagents over 30+ tools and machine-learning endpoints, mixes structured database access with sandboxed code for genome-scale analyses, and applies claim-level multi-model reconciliation before editorial assembly.
  We evaluate BioResearcher across unit-level capabilities, open-ended biomedical reasoning, and end-to-end clinical discovery. It leads evaluated baselines on 109 single-step tests (83.49% pass rate; 0.892 average score), achieves strong biomedical benchmark performance (89.33% on BixBench-Verified-50 and the top 0.758 mean score on BaisBench Scientific Discovery), and leads on a 30-query clinical end-to-end benchmark with the highest positive hit rate (74.7% $\pm$ 3.3%) and negative clear rate (96.8% $\pm$ 0.2%). These results show broad, competitive performance across unit-level, open-ended, and end-to-end clinical evaluations.

</details>


### [127] [Who Prices Cognitive Labor in the Age of Agents? A Position on Compute-Anchored Wages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05558)
*Siqi Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文驳斥了"AI智能体因可零成本复制而压低认知劳动工资"的直觉，认为智能体本质是生产技术而非劳动，其将计算资本转化为有效认知劳动；工资的定价权从劳动市场转移到计算资本市场


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠正关于AI智能体经济学的主流直觉谬误：该直觉认为智能体因边际成本近零而具有无限弹性供给，从而将认知劳动工资压至零

Method: 在教科书要素定价框架（Mankiw 2020）基础上，通过CES聚合分离替代性与互补性任务，构建了计算锚定工资（CAW）模型

Result: 替代性任务中人类工资受限于λ·k·r_c（相对生产率×计算强度×计算资本租赁率）；在互补性任务中呈现技能偏向型技术变革的方向性反转

Conclusion: 计算资本市场取代劳动市场成为认知劳动的定价者

Abstract: A natural intuition about the economics of AI agents is that, because agents can be replicated at near-zero marginal cost, they constitute a labor input in infinitely elastic supply, and therefore drive cognitive-labor wages to zero. We argue this framing is wrong in mechanism but partially correct in conclusion, and that the correction matters for both theory and policy. \textbf{Agents are not labor; they are a production technology that converts compute capital $K_c$ into effective units of cognitive labor $L_A$.} Once this is recognized, the elastic-supply margin that anchors the equilibrium wage migrates from the labor market to the compute capital market. Building on the textbook factor-pricing framework \citep{mankiw2020}, we derive a \emph{Compute-Anchored Wage} (CAW) bound stating that, on tasks where human and agent cognitive labor are substitutes, the competitive human wage is bounded above by $λ\cdot k \cdot r_c$, where $r_c$ is the rental rate of compute capital, $k$ is the compute intensity of one effective agent-labor unit, and $λ$ is the relative human-to-agent productivity. We generalize the result through CES aggregation, separate substitutable from complementary tasks (yielding a directional inversion of skill-biased technical change), and discuss factor-share consequences. The position is concise: \emph{the price-setter for cognitive labor is no longer the labor market.}

</details>


### [128] [From Coordinate Matching to Structural Alignment: Rethinking Prototype Alignment in Heterogeneous Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05959)
*Xinghao Wu,Jianwei Niu,Guogang Zhu,Xuefeng Liu,Shaojie Tang,Jiayuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对异构联邦学习中坐标对齐机制的局限性，提出结构对齐方法FedSAF，通过对齐类间关系结构而非绝对坐标来提升性能，实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有原型方法采用坐标对齐，假设所有客户端映射到单一全局子空间。该假设在异构FL中不合理，因为强制异构特征提取器在共享子空间优化会限制学习能力。

Method: 设计结构对齐机制，将对齐目标从绝对坐标转向类间关系结构，使客户端保持各自特征子空间同时学习一致的类间语义关系。

Result: 结构对齐在异构设置下始终优于坐标对齐，FedSAF在多个基准上超越SOTA原型方法达3.52%提升。

Conclusion: FedSAF通过结构对齐优于坐标对齐，在多个基准上提升最高3.52%，验证了关系结构对齐对异构联邦学习的有效性。

Abstract: Heterogeneous federated learning (HtFL) aims to enable collaboration among clients that differ in both data distributions and model architectures. Prototype-based methods, which communicate class-level feature centers (prototypes) instead of full model parameters, have recently shown strong potential for HtFL. Existing prototype-based HtFL methods typically reuse the MSE-based or cosine-based alignment mechanism developed for homogeneous FL when aligning client-specific representations with global prototypes. These approaches are essentially coordinate alignment, where representations of clients are forced to match the global prototypes in the embedding space in an element-wise manner. Such alignment implicitly assumes that all clients should map their representations into the feature subspace defined by the global prototypes. This assumption is reasonable in homogeneous FL, where all clients share the same feature extractor. However, it becomes problematic in HtFL, since heterogeneous feature extractors naturally induce client-specific feature subspaces, and forcing all clients to optimize within a single global subspace unnecessarily suppresses their learning capacity. We observe that coordinate alignment implicitly couples two distinct objectives: aligning inter-class semantic structure, which is directly beneficial for classification, and enforcing a shared feature basis, which is unnecessary and even harmful under model heterogeneity. Building on this insight, we design FedSAF, which shifts the alignment objective from absolute coordinates to inter-class relational structure. We demonstrate that structural alignment consistently outperforms coordinate alignment in heterogeneous settings. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that our structural alignment outperforms state-of-the-art prototype-based HtFL methods by up to 3.52\%.

</details>


### [129] [VibeServe: Can AI Agents Build Bespoke LLM Serving Systems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06068)
*Keisuke Kamahori,Shihang Li,Simon Peter,Baris Kasikci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VibeServe是一个多智能体循环系统，能够自动为不同使用场景合成定制的LLM服务技术栈。在标准场景下与vLLM性能持平，在非标准场景下通过利用通用系统忽视的优化机会实现超越，验证了"生成时特化"这一新型基础设施软件设计范式的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM服务系统的构建方式存在局限性：采用单一通用技术栈，通过工程师长时间手工调优来支持所有模型和工作负载。这种方法不仅耗时耗力，而且对于非标准场景（如特殊模型架构、特定工作负载特征、硬件特定优化需求）难以充分发挥优化潜力。
